\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-(m+1)/2-12(m+21/24)\varepsilon}+\kappa\rho^{\varepsilon}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle+\text{T}

(4.19)

with

\begin{split}\text{T}&=(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\int_{|x-y|\leq\ell_{1},|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}}dydy^{\prime}f^{2}_{\ell}(y-y^{\prime})\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times\langle J(y^{\prime})a_{y^{\prime}}\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)J(y)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We decompose
\text{T}=\text{S}_{1}+\text{S}_{2}
, with

\begin{split}\text{S}_{1}&=(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\int_{|x-y||,|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}}dydy^{\prime}(f_{\ell}^{2}(y-y^{\prime})-1)\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\langle J(y^{\prime})a_{y^{\prime}}\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)J(y)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,,\\
\text{S}_{2}&=(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-y|\leq\ell_{1}\\
|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}\end{subarray}}dydy^{\prime}\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\langle J(y^{\prime})a_{y^{\prime}}\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m}(w)J(y)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

To control
\text{S}_{1}
, we observe that, with Lemma
2.1
,

1-f_{\ell}^{2}(y-y^{\prime})\leq 2\omega_{\ell}(y-y^{\prime})\leq\frac{C\chi(|y-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell)}{|y-y^{\prime}|}\,.

(4.20)

Therefore, recalling that
\ell=C\rho^{-\delta}
,

\begin{split}|\text{S}_{1}|\leq\;&C\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\int_{|w-y|,|w-y^{\prime}|\leq R+\ell_{1}}dydy^{\prime}\,\frac{\chi(|y-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell)}{|y-y^{\prime}|}|\nu(x-y^{\prime})|^{2}\|a_{y}\mathcal{N}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi\|^{2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{3/2-2\delta}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)\mathcal{N}_{R+\ell_{1}}(w)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

With Lemma
4.2
and using the translation invariance of
\Psi
, we obtain

\begin{split}|\text{S}_{1}|\leq\;&C\rho^{3/2-2\delta}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq\kappa\rho^{\varepsilon}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\end{split}

(4.21)

for any
\kappa>0
, if
\rho>0
is small enough (and
\delta,\varepsilon>0
are sufficiently small).

As for
\text{S}_{2}
, we write

\begin{split}S_{2}=&(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-y|\leq\ell_{1}\\
|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}\end{subarray}}dydy^{\prime}\chi(|w-y|\leq R+\ell_{1})\chi(|w-y^{\prime}|\leq R+\ell_{1})\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\langle J(y^{\prime})a_{y^{\prime}}\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m}(w)J(y)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We can now remove the constraints
|x-y|,|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}
. The contributions arising from
|x-y|>\ell_{1}
or
|x-y^{\prime}|>\ell_{1}
can be handled as we did above with the term
A
. We find

0\leq\text{S}_{2}\leq\kappa\rho^{\varepsilon}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle+\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}

(4.22)

for any
\kappa>0
, if
\rho
is small enough, with

\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}=(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\int dydy^{\prime}\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\langle\Xi_{y},\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m}(w)\Xi_{y^{\prime}}\rangle

(4.23)

where we defined
\Xi_{y}=\chi(|w-y|\leq R+\ell_{1})J(y)a_{y}\Psi
. Since

\int dydy^{\prime}\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\langle\Xi_{y},\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)\Xi_{y^{\prime}}\rangle\geq 0

for every
x\in\Lambda
, we can estimate

\begin{split}0\leq\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}&\leq(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int dydy^{\prime}(\nu*\nu)(y-y^{\prime})\,\langle\Xi_{y},\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m}(w)\Xi_{y^{\prime}}\rangle\,.\end{split}

Switching to Fourier space, we obtain

\begin{split}0\leq\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}\leq\frac{(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\widehat{\nu}_{k}^{2}\,\langle\widehat{\Xi}_{k},\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)\widehat{\Xi}_{k}\rangle\,.\end{split}

From (
2.29
), we find

\|\widehat{\gamma}\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1+\|\widehat{\sigma}\|_{\infty}\lesssim 1+\|\sigma\|_{1}\lesssim\rho^{-\varepsilon/2}

which implies that
\widehat{\nu}_{k}^{2}=\widehat{\sigma}_{k}^{2}/\widehat{\gamma}_{k}^{2}=1-1/\widehat{\gamma}_{k}^{2}\leq 1-C\rho^{\varepsilon}
, for every momentum
k\in\Lambda^{*}
. Hence, adjusting slightly the constant
C>0
to absorb the error proportional to
\rho^{3\varepsilon/2}
,

0\leq\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}\leq\frac{\big(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon}\big)}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\langle\widehat{\Xi}_{k},\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)\widehat{\Xi}_{k}\rangle\,.

Switching back to position space, we obtain

\begin{split}0\leq\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}&\leq\big(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon}\big)\int_{|w-y|\leq R+\ell_{1}}dy\langle a_{y}\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\end{split}

and therefore, with Lemma
4.2
,

\begin{split}0\leq\widetilde{\text{S}}_{2}&\leq(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon})\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m}_{R}(w)\mathcal{N}_{R+\ell_{1}}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon})(1+C\ell_{1}/R)\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon})\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Here we used the observation that
\ell_{1}/R=C\rho^{5\varepsilon/4}\ll\rho^{\varepsilon}
.
From (
4.22
), choosing
\kappa>0
small enough, we find

\text{S}_{2}\leq(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon})\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle

for an appropriate constant
C>0
. Combining this bound with (
4.21
) and (
4.19
), we conclude that

\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-(m+1)/2-12(m+21/24)\varepsilon}+(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon})\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle

which implies that

\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-(m+1)/2-12(m+1)\varepsilon}

for a constant
C>0
depending only on
m\in\mathbb{N}
.

Next, we prove (
4.5
). Since the operators
b_{x},b_{x}^{*}
satisfy the same canonical commutation relations as the operators
a_{x},a_{x}^{*}
and since we have the same bound (
4.9
) for
\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}(w)
, as we did for
\mathcal{N}_{R}(w)
, we can proceed similarly as in the proof of (
4.4
). In the following, we only highlight the differences.

As above, we proceed by induction over
m\in\mathbb{N}
. Since the claim is trivial for
m=0
, we can assume (
4.5
) to hold true for some
m\in\mathbb{N}
. Similarly to (
4.13
), we compute

\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-10m\varepsilon}+\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\,\langle b_{x}\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m}(w)b_{x}\Psi\rangle\,.

Instead of (
2.34
), we apply now the identity

\begin{split}b_{x}\Psi=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}(J(x)-1)\Psi+\int dy\,\nu(x-y)J(x)a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\\
=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}(J(x)-1)\Psi+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dy\,\nu(x-y)J(x)(J(y)-1)\Psi\\
&+\int dy\,\nu(x-y)J(x)b_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\end{split}

(4.24)

which follows from (
2.34
), the definition (
4.2
) and from the fact that
\int dy\,\nu(y)=0
(recall the notation
\nu=\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma
). By Cauchy-Schwarz, we arrive at

\begin{split}\langle\Psi,&\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m+1}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{-10m\varepsilon}+C\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2}\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dx\langle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi,(J(x)-1)^{2}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2}\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dxdydy^{\prime}\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\langle\Psi,(J(y)-1)\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m}(w)(J(y^{\prime})-1)\Psi\rangle\\
&+(1+C\rho^{3\varepsilon/2})\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dxdydy^{\prime}\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y^{\prime})\,\langle\Psi,J(y)b_{y}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m}(w)b_{y^{\prime}}^{*}J(y^{\prime})\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

(4.25)

With (
2.10
), we can bound the second term by

\begin{split}C&\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2}\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dxdy\,\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2}\int_{|y-w|\leq R+\ell}dxdy\,\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m/2}_{R}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2}\ell^{2}\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}^{m+1}_{R+\ell}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2-2\delta}\,\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq\kappa\rho^{\varepsilon}\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\end{split}

for any
\kappa>0
, if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
and
\rho>0
are small enough. Here, we used (
4.9
).

Up to errors arising from the restriction of the integrals to
|x-y|,|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}
(which can be bounded similarly to (
4.17
)), with Cauchy-Schwarz we can estimate the third term on the r.h.s. of (
4.25
) by

\begin{split}C&\rho^{1-3\varepsilon/2}\int_{|x-w|\leq R,|x-y^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{1}}dxdydy^{\prime}dz\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y^{\prime}-z)\langle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m/2}(w)\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m/2}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2-3\varepsilon/2}\ell^{2}R^{3}\int_{|z-w|\leq R+\ell_{1}+\ell}dz\langle\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m/2}(w)\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m/2}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{7/2-3\varepsilon/2-2\delta}R^{6}\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m}(w)\Psi\rangle+C\rho^{5/2-3\varepsilon/2-2\delta}R^{3}\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R+\ell_{1}+\ell}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{-10m\varepsilon}+\kappa\rho^{\varepsilon}\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta,\rho>0
are small enough. Here, we wrote
a_{z}=b_{z}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}},a_{z}^{*}=b_{z}^{*}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}
and we applied the induction assumption.

The last term on the r.h.s. of (
4.25
) can be bounded exactly as we did with the last term on the r.h.s. of (
4.14
). Taking into account that the contribution arising from the commutator
[b_{y},b_{y^{\prime}}^{*}]=\delta(y-y^{\prime})
(analogous to the second term on the r.h.s. of (
4.15
)) can be bounded, with the induction assumption, by

\begin{split}\int_{|x-w|\leq R}dxdy\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}\langle J(y)\Psi,&(\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}(w)+1)^{m}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq C\rho^{3/2}R^{3}\langle\Psi,(\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}(w)+1)^{m}\Psi\rangle\leq\rho^{-9\varepsilon-10m\varepsilon}\end{split}

we arrive at

\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-10m\varepsilon-9\varepsilon}+(1-C\rho^{\varepsilon})\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\,,

which leads to

\langle\Psi,\widetilde{\mathcal{N}}_{R}^{m+1}(w)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-10(m+1)\varepsilon}\,.

∎

Lemma
4.1
gives us control on localized monomials of arbitrary degree, with matching numbers of creation and annihilation operators.

Lemma 4.3
.

Let
R=C\rho^{-1/2-3\varepsilon}
,
k\in\mathbb{N}\backslash\{0\}
. Then we have

\int dx_{1}\dots dx_{k}\,\prod_{j=2}^{k}\chi(|x_{1}-x_{j}|\leq R)\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\dots a_{x_{k}}^{*}a_{x_{k}}\dots a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-(k-3)/2-(12k-9)\varepsilon}L^{3}

(4.26)

and

\int dx_{1}\dots dx_{k}\,\prod_{j=2}^{k}\chi(|x_{1}-x_{j}|\leq R)\langle\Psi,b_{x_{1}}^{*}\dots b_{x_{k}}^{*}b_{x_{k}}\dots b_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{3/2-(10k-9)\varepsilon}L^{3}

(4.27)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
and
\rho>0
are small enough (if
k=1
, we remove
\prod_{j=2}^{k}\chi(|x_{1}-x_{j}|\leq R)
).

Proof.

To show (
4.26
), we write

\begin{split}\int dx_{1}&\dots dx_{k}\,\prod_{j=2}^{k}\chi(|x_{1}-x_{j}|\leq R)\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\dots a_{x_{k}}^{*}a_{x_{k}}\dots a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\\
&=\int dx_{1}\dots dx_{k-1}\,\prod_{j=2}^{k-1}\chi(|x_{1}-x_{j}|\leq R)\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\dots a_{x_{k-1}}^{*}\mathcal{N}_{R}(x_{1})a_{x_{k-1}}\dots a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Iterating
k
times, we find

\begin{split}\int dx_{1}&\dots dx_{k}\,\prod_{j=2}^{k}\chi(|x_{1}-x_{j}|\leq R)\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\dots a_{x_{k}}^{*}a_{x_{k}}\dots a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq\int dx_{1}\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\mathcal{N}^{k-1}_{R}(x_{1})a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq CR^{-3}\int dzdx_{1}\chi(|x_{1}-z|\leq R)\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\mathcal{N}^{k-1}_{R}(x_{1})a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq CR^{-3}\int dzdx_{1}\chi(|x_{1}-z|\leq R)\langle\Psi,a_{x_{1}}^{*}\mathcal{N}^{k-1}_{2R}(z)a_{x_{1}}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq CR^{-3}\int dz\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{k}_{2R}(z)\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{-(k-3)/2-(12k-9)\varepsilon}L^{3}\,,\end{split}

where we used (
4.4
). Since the operators
b^{*}_{x},b_{x}
satisfy the same commutation relations as the operators
a_{x}^{*},a_{x}
, (
4.27
) can be proven analogously, using (
4.5
) instead of (
4.4
) in the last step.
∎

It will also be useful to bound the expectation of a single creation or annihilation operator. To this end, we state the following lemma.

Lemma 4.4
.

We assume
\varepsilon,\delta>0
and
\rho>0
to be small enough. Then, we have

\Big|\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dx\langle\Psi,a_{x}\Psi\rangle-\rho_{0}L^{3}\Big|=\Big|\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dx\langle\Psi,b_{x}\Psi\rangle\Big|\lesssim\rho^{2-9\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}

(4.28)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
and
\rho>0
are small enough.

Proof.

From (
4.24
), we recall

b_{x}\Psi=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}(J(x)-1)\Psi+\int dy\,\nu(x-y)f_{\ell}(x-y)a_{y}^{*}J(x)J(y)

with the notation
\nu=\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma
. Since
\int\nu(x)dx=0
, we obtain

\begin{split}\Big|\sqrt{\rho_{0}}&\int dx\langle\Psi,b_{x}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\lesssim\;&\rho_{0}\int dx\langle\Psi,(1-J(x))\Psi\rangle+\Big|\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nu(x-y)f_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}(J(x)-1)J(y)\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&+\Big|\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nu(x-y)(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\Big|\,.\end{split}

(4.29)

With (
2.10
),
\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\lesssim\ell^{2}
and (
4.6
), we can bound the first term by

\rho\int dx\langle\Psi,(1-J(x))\Psi\rangle\leq\rho\int dxdy\,\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2-3\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\,.

(4.30)

To estimate the third term on the r.h.s. of (
4.29
), we use
\|\nu\|_{\infty}\lesssim\rho
and again
\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\lesssim\ell^{2}
and (
4.6
). We find

\begin{split}\Big|\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,&\nu(x-y)(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3/2}\ell^{2}\int dy\,\|a_{y}\Psi\|\lesssim\rho^{3/2-2\delta}L^{3/2}\Big[\int dy\,\|a_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\leq\rho^{2-3\varepsilon/2-2\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

As for the second term on the r.h.s. of (
4.29
) we find, using (
2.27
) to restrict to
|x-y|\leq R
and applying Cauchy-Schwarz,

\begin{split}\Big|&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nu(x-y)f_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}(1-J(x))J(y)\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\sqrt{\rho}\Big[\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle a_{y}\Psi,(1-J(x))a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\Big[\int dxdy\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}\langle\Psi,(1-J(x))\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{5/4}\Big[\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydz\,\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\Big[\int dxdz\,\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{5/4-2\delta}\Big[\int_{|z-y|\leq 2R}dydz\,\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\Big[\int dz\,\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2-9\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

where we used (
4.6
) and (
4.26
) with
k=2
. This completes the proof of (
4.28
).
∎

Comparing (
4.4
) with (
4.5
) or (
4.26
) with (
4.27
), we observe that the estimates improve, when we factor out the Bose-Einstein condensate generated by the Weyl operator
W(\rho_{0})
, switching from the original creation and annihilation operators
a^{*},a_{x}
to the operators
b_{x}^{*},b_{x}
. In fact, we can derive even stronger bounds, if we also factor out the excitations of the condensate generated in (
2.33
) by the Bogoliubov transformation
T
. For
x\in\Lambda
, we define the new creation and annihilation operators

\begin{split}c^{*}_{x}&=b^{*}(\gamma_{x})-b(\sigma_{x})=a^{*}(\gamma_{x})-a(\sigma_{x})-\sqrt{\rho_{0}}=TW(\rho_{0})a^{*}_{x}W^{*}(\rho_{0})T^{*}\\
c_{x}&=b(\gamma_{x})-b^{*}(\sigma_{x})=a(\gamma_{x})-a^{*}(\sigma_{x})-\sqrt{\rho_{0}}=TW(\rho_{0})a_{x}W^{*}(\rho_{0})T^{*}\,.\end{split}

(4.31)

Lemma 4.5
.

We have

\int dx\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{2-16\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\,.

(4.32)

Moreover, let
R=C\rho^{-1/2-3\varepsilon}
. Then, we have

\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}c_{y}^{*}c_{y}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{2-30\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.

(4.33)

More generally, for
\varphi,\tau\in\{\sigma,\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma,\gamma-\mathbbm{1},\mathbbm{1}\}
, we find (if
\tau=\mathbbm{1}
, we set
c(\tau_{x})=c_{x}
)

\int dx\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{2-22\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}

(4.34)

and

\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}(\varphi_{y})c(\varphi_{y})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{2-42\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.

(4.35)

Remark.
the r.h.s. of (
4.33
) should be compared with the r.h.s. of (
4.26
), (
4.27
), with
k=2
.

Proof.

To prove this lemma, it will be convenient to express the operators
c,c^{*}
in terms of the new fields

\begin{split}e_{x}&=c(\gamma^{-1}_{x})=a_{x}-a^{*}(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma_{x})-\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\\
e^{*}_{x}&=c^{*}(\gamma^{-1}_{x})=a^{*}_{x}-a(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma_{x})-\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\end{split}

(4.36)

(where we used the observation
\widehat{\gamma^{-1}}(0)=1/\widehat{\gamma}(0)=1/\sqrt{1-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}(0)}=1
) and to treat
e_{x},e_{x}^{*}
as a small perturbation of

\begin{split}g_{x}&=a_{x}-a^{*}(k_{x})-\sqrt{\rho_{0}},\\
g_{x}^{*}&=a_{x}^{*}-a(k_{x})-\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\end{split}

(4.37)

with the kernel
k_{x}(y)=f_{\ell}(x-y)(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma)(x-y)
. To control the difference between (
4.36
) and (
4.37
), we notice that

e_{x}=g_{x}+a^{*}(\xi_{x}),

(4.38)

with
\xi_{x}(y)=\xi(x-y)
and
\xi(y)=k(y)-(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma)(y)=(f_{\ell}(y)-1)(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma)(y)
.
From Lemma
2.1
and Lemma
2.2
, we find

\|k\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2},\quad\|\xi\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2+\delta},\quad\|\xi\|_{1}\lesssim\rho^{1-\delta}\,.

(4.39)

The advantage of working with the operators (
4.37
) is clear from the identity

g_{x}\Psi=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}(J(x)-1)\Psi+\int dy\,k(x-y)(J(x)J(y)-1)\Psi

(4.40)

which follows from (
2.34
) and from the intertwining relations (
2.9
). The appearance of the differences
J(x)-1
in the first and
J(x)J(y)-1
in the second term are crucial to get improved bounds, compared with the estimates in Lemma
4.1
.

We show first how to bound (
4.32
), (
4.33
) in terms of quantities defined through the fields (
4.37
). With
c_{x}=e(\gamma_{x})
, using
\gamma*\gamma=1+\sigma*\sigma
and the bound
\|\sigma*\sigma\|_{1}\lesssim\|\sigma\|_{1}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{-\varepsilon}
from Lemma
2.2
, we obtain

\begin{split}\int dx\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}c_{x}\Psi\rangle&=\int dydy^{\prime}\,(\gamma*\gamma)(y-y^{\prime})\langle\Psi,e_{y}^{*}e_{y^{\prime}}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{-\varepsilon}\int dx\langle\Psi,e_{x}^{*}e_{x}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

From (
4.38
), we have

\int dx\,\langle\Psi,e_{x}^{*}e_{x}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\int dx\langle\Psi,g_{x}^{*}g_{x}\Psi\rangle+\int dx\langle\Psi,a(\xi_{x})a^{*}(\xi_{x})\Psi\rangle\,.

With (
4.39
) and (
4.6
), we can estimate

\begin{split}\int dx\langle\Psi,a(\xi_{x})a^{*}(\xi_{x})\Psi\rangle&=\int dx\,\|\xi_{x}\|_{2}^{2}+\int dydy^{\prime}\,(\xi*\xi)(y-y^{\prime})\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{y^{\prime}}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2+\delta}L^{3}+\rho^{2-2\delta}\int dy\,\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. We conclude that

\int dx\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{-\varepsilon}\int dx\langle\Psi,g_{x}^{*}g_{x}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{2-\varepsilon}L^{3}\,.

(4.41)

To express (
4.33
) in terms of the fields
g_{x},g_{x}^{*}
we proceed similarly. First, we write

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\|c_{x}c_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&=\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}e^{*}(\gamma_{y})e(\gamma_{y})c_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}e_{y}^{*}e_{y}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})(y-z)(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})(y-z^{\prime})\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}e^{*}_{z}e_{z^{\prime}}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Using the decay of the kernel
(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})
, as established in (
2.27
), we can restrict the integrals to
|x-z|,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq 2R
. Contributions from
|x-z|>2R
or
|x-z^{\prime}|>2R
(which imply that
|y-z|>R
or
|y-z^{\prime}|>R
) can be controlled similarly as the term (
4.16
) (here, we additionally need to express
c_{x}^{*},e_{z}^{*},e_{z^{\prime}},c_{x}
in terms of the fields
a^{*},a
, recalling the definitions (
4.31
), (
4.36
)), to be able to apply Lemma
4.1
to estimate the resulting expectations). We find

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|c_{x}c_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}e_{y}^{*}e_{y}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int_{|x-y|\leq R,|x-z|,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq 2R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})(y-z)(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})(y-z^{\prime})\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}e^{*}_{z}e_{z^{\prime}}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{-3\varepsilon}\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdz\langle\Psi,c_{x}^{*}e^{*}_{z}e_{z}c_{x}\Psi\rangle\end{split}

where we used Cauchy-Schwarz and the estimate
\|(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})*(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})\|_{1}\lesssim\|\gamma-\mathbbm{1}\|_{1}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{-3\varepsilon}
. Noticing that
[c_{x},e_{z}]=0
, we can move
c_{x}^{*},c_{x}
in the middle of the product and repeat the same argument. We conclude that

\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|c_{x}c_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{-6\varepsilon}\int_{|z-w|<R}dzdw\,\|e_{z}e_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\,.

(4.42)

To switch to the
g,g^{*}
operators, we recall (
4.38
). We find

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|e_{x}e_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,e_{x}^{*}g_{y}^{*}g_{y}e_{x}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,e_{x}^{*}a(\xi_{y})a^{*}(\xi_{y})e_{x}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

With
[e_{x}^{*},g_{y}^{*}]=-\langle\xi_{x},k_{y}\rangle
,
[e_{x}^{*},a^{*}(\xi_{y})]=-\langle\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma_{x},\xi_{y}\rangle
,
[a(\xi_{y}),a^{*}(\xi_{y})]=\|\xi_{y}\|^{2}
, with (
4.39
) and since, additionally,

|\langle\xi_{x},k_{y}\rangle|,|\langle\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma_{x},\xi_{y}\rangle|\lesssim\rho^{7/4+\delta/2}

(4.43)

we conclude that

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|e_{x}e_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,g_{y}^{*}e_{x}^{*}e_{x}g_{y}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|x-y|<R}dxdy\langle\Psi,a^{*}(\xi_{y})e_{x}^{*}e_{x}a(\xi_{y})\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho^{1/2-9\varepsilon+\delta}\int dx\langle\Psi,e_{x}^{*}e_{x}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{2-9\varepsilon+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(4.44)

To bound the first term, we apply (
4.38
) again. We find

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle&\Psi,g_{y}^{*}e_{x}^{*}e_{x}g_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,g_{y}^{*}g_{x}^{*}g_{x}g_{y}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,g_{y}^{*}a(\xi_{x})a^{*}(\xi_{x})g_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Proceeding as we did above, using again the bounds (
4.39
), (
4.43
), and combining with (
4.44
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|e_{x}e_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,g_{y}^{*}g_{x}^{*}g_{x}g_{y}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,a^{*}(\xi_{x})\big(e_{y}^{*}e_{y}+g_{y}^{*}g_{y}\big)a(\xi_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho^{1/2-9\varepsilon+\delta}\int dy\langle\Psi,g_{y}^{*}g_{y}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{2-9\varepsilon+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(4.45)

In the second term, we estimate

e_{y}^{*}e_{y}\lesssim a_{y}^{*}a_{y}+a^{*}(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma_{y})a(\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma_{y})+\rho

(4.46)

and similarly for
g_{y}^{*}g_{y}
(with
\gamma^{-1}\ast\sigma
replaced by
k
). To bound the resulting contribution

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,&\langle\Psi,a^{*}(\xi_{x})a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a(\xi_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&=\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,\xi(x-z)\xi(x-z^{\prime})\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{z^{\prime}}\Psi\rangle\end{split}

we use the fast decay of
\xi
, to restrict the integrals over
z,z^{\prime}
to
|x-z|,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq R
, with only small errors. For example, consider the contribution

\begin{split}\text{A}&=\int_{|x-y|\leq R,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq R,|x-z|\geq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|\xi(x-z)||\xi(x-z^{\prime})|\,\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{y}a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{1+\delta}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{3}:|n|\geq 2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-y|\leq R,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq R,\\
|x-z-nR/2|\leq R/2\end{subarray}}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\frac{\rho^{3k\varepsilon/2}}{|n|^{k}}\|a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\|\|a_{z^{\prime}}a_{y}\Psi\|\\
&\lesssim\rho^{1+3\varepsilon k/2+\delta}\sum_{|n|\geq 2}|n|^{-k}\int dx\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}(x)\mathcal{N}_{R/2}(x-nR/2)\Psi\rangle^{1/2}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}^{2}_{R}(x)\Psi\rangle^{1/2}\,.\end{split}

By the translation invariance of
\Psi
and Lemma
4.1
, we find that

\begin{split}|\text{A}|&\lesssim\rho^{1+3\varepsilon k/2+\delta}L^{3}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}_{R}(0)^{2}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{1+3\varepsilon k/2+\delta}\rho^{-1-24\varepsilon}L^{3}\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
k
is chosen large enough. We conclude, with Lemma
4.3
and recalling (
4.39
), that

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,a^{*}(\xi_{x})a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a(\xi_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|x-y|\leq R\\
|x-z|\leq R\\
|x-z^{\prime}|\leq R\end{subarray}}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,\xi(x-z)\xi(x-z^{\prime})\,\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{x}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int dydzdz^{\prime}\,\chi(|y-z^{\prime}|\leq 2R)(|\xi|*|\xi|)(z-z^{\prime})\chi(|y-z|\leq 2R)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\||\xi|*|\xi|\|_{1}\int_{|y-z|\leq 2R}dydz\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{2-2\delta}\rho^{1/2-15\varepsilon}L^{3}\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. The other contributions from (
4.46
) can be bounded similarly. Taking into account (
4.42
), we arrive at

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\|c_{x}c_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{-6\varepsilon}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\psi,g_{y}^{*}g_{x}^{*}g_{x}g_{y}\psi\rangle+\rho^{1/2-15\varepsilon+\delta}\int dy\langle\Psi,g_{y}^{*}g_{y}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{2-15\varepsilon+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

With (
4.41
) and the last equation, to complete the proof of the lemma it is enough to show

\begin{split}\int dx\langle\Psi,g_{x}^{*}g_{x}\Psi\rangle&\lesssim\rho^{2-15\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\\
\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\langle\Psi,g_{x}^{*}g_{y}^{*}g_{y}g_{x}\Psi\rangle&\lesssim\rho^{2-24\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(4.47)

To this end, we use (
4.40
) to estimate

\begin{split}&\int dx\,\langle\Psi,g_{x}^{*}g_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho\int dx\,\|(J(x)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\int dxdzdz^{\prime}k(x-z)k(x-z^{\prime})\langle a_{z}^{*}(J(x)J(z)-1)\Psi,a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}(J(x)J(z^{\prime})-1)\Psi\rangle=\text{T}_{1}+\text{T}_{2}\,.\end{split}

To bound the first term, we observe that, from (
2.10
),

(1-J(x))^{2}\leq 1-J(x)\lesssim\int dy\,\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\,.

(4.48)

With Lemma
2.1
and (
4.6
), we obtain

\text{T}_{1}\lesssim\rho^{2-3\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\,.

To control
\text{T}_{2}
, we commute
a_{z}
and
a^{*}_{z^{\prime}}
. We find

\begin{split}\text{T}_{2}\lesssim\;&\int dxdzdz^{\prime}\,k(x-z)k(x-z^{\prime})\langle a_{z^{\prime}}(J(x)J(z)-1)\Psi,a_{z}(J(x)J(z^{\prime})-1)\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdz\,|k(x-z)|^{2}\|(J(x)J(z)-1)\Psi\|^{2}=\text{T}_{21}+\text{T}_{22}\,.\end{split}

Writing
J(x)J(z)-1=(J(x)-1)J(z)+(J(z)-1)
, we find, since
\|k\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2}
,

\text{T}_{22}\leq\int dxdz|k(x-z)|^{2}\,\|(J(x)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{5/2-3\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Furthermore, using the fast decay (
2.27
) of
k(x)
for
|x|>R
and applying Cauchy-Schwarz, we obtain

\begin{split}\text{T}_{21}&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdzdz^{\prime}|k(x-z^{\prime})|^{2}\|a_{z}(J(x)J(z^{\prime})-1)\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdzdz^{\prime}|k(x-z^{\prime})|^{2}\|(J(x)J(z^{\prime})f_{\ell}(x-z)f_{\ell}(z-z^{\prime})-1)a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\,.\end{split}

Decomposing

\begin{split}J(x)J(z^{\prime})&f_{\ell}(x-z)f_{\ell}(z-z^{\prime})-1\\
&=J(x)J(z^{\prime})(f_{\ell}(x-z)f_{\ell}(x-z^{\prime})-1)+(J(z^{\prime})-1)J(x)+(J(x)-1)\end{split}

(4.49)

we can bound the integral on the r.h.s. of
\text{T}_{21}
by the sum of several terms. Let us focus on the term
\text{T}_{211}
, proportional to
J(x)-1
. With (
4.48
), we find

\begin{split}\text{T}_{211}\lesssim\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdz&dz^{\prime}|k(x-z^{\prime})|^{2}\|(J(x)-1)a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3/2}\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdzdw\,\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3/2}\ell^{2}\int_{|z-w|\leq 2R}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\leq\rho^{2-15\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

where we applied Lemma
4.3
. The other contributions from (
4.49
) can be handled similarly. This leads to the first bound in (
4.47
).

To prove the second bound, we go back to (
4.40
) and we apply
a_{y}
. With (
2.9
) and
[a_{y},a_{z}^{*}]=\delta(y-z)
, we obtain

\begin{split}a_{y}g_{x}\Psi=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,(J(x)-1)a_{y}\Psi+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)J(x)a_{y}\Psi+k(x-y)(J(x)J(y)-1)\Psi\\
&+\int dz\,k(x-z)a_{z}^{*}(J(x)J(z)-1)a_{y}\Psi\\
&+\int dz\,k(x-z)(f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)a_{z}^{*}J(x)J(z)a_{y}\Psi\,.\end{split}

On the r.h.s., we replace
a_{y}=g_{y}+a^{*}(k_{y})+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}
. Afterwards, we use (
2.9
) to commute
a^{*}(k_{y})
to the left of the operators
J(x),J(z)
(after expanding
a^{*}(k_{y})=\int dz\,k(y-z)a_{z}^{*}
). At this point, we subtract factors
a^{*}(k_{y})g_{x}\Psi
and
\sqrt{\rho_{0}}g_{x}\Psi
(using (
4.40
), we get four terms, canceling contributions in
a_{y}g_{x}\Psi
). We find

\begin{split}g_{y}g_{x}\Psi=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,(J(x)-1)g_{y}\Psi+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,\int dz\,k(y-z)(f_{\ell}(x-z)-1)a_{z}^{*}J(x)\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)J(x)g_{y}\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)\int dz\,k(y-z)f_{\ell}(z-x)a_{z}^{*}J(x)\Psi\\
&+\rho_{0}\,(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)J(x)\Psi+k(x-y)(J(x)J(y)-1)\Psi\\
&+\int dz\,k(x-z)a_{z}^{*}(J(x)J(z)-1)g_{y}\Psi\\
&+\int dzdw\,k(x-z)k(y-w)(f_{\ell}(w-x)f_{\ell}(w-z)-1)a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}J(x)J(z)\Psi\\
&+\int dz\,k(x-z)(f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)a_{z}^{*}J(x)J(z)g_{y}\Psi\\
&+\int dzdw\,k(x-z)k(y-w)f_{\ell}(x-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)(f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}J(x)J(z)\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dz\,k(x-z)(f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)a_{z}^{*}J(x)J(z)\Psi\,.\end{split}

We apply again (
4.40
) to compute
g_{y}\Psi
and, again, we commute creation operators to the left of all
J
-factors. After a long but straightforward computation, we arrive at

\begin{split}g_{y}g_{x}\Psi=\;&\rho_{0}\,(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi+\rho_{0}\,(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)J(x)J(y)\Psi\\
&+k(x-y)(J(x)J(y)-1)\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dz\,k(y-z)(f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(x-z)-1)a_{z}^{*}J(x)J(y)J(z)\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dz\,k(x-z)(f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(y-z)-1)a_{z}^{*}J(x)J(y)J(z)\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dz\,k(y-z)a_{z}^{*}(J(x)-1)(J(y)J(z)-1)\Psi\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dz\,k(x-z)a_{z}^{*}(J(x)J(z)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\\
&+\int dzdw\,k(x-z)k(y-w)(f_{\ell}(x-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}J(x)J(z)J(y)J(w)\Psi\\
&+\int dzdw\,k(x-z)k(y-w)a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}(J(x)J(z)-1)(J(y)J(w)-1)\Psi\\
=:\,&\sum_{j=1}^{9}A^{(j)}_{x,y}\Psi\,.\end{split}

(4.50)

To bound the first contribution, we observe that, similarly to (
4.48
),

\begin{split}(J(x)&-1)^{2}(J(y)-1)^{2}\\
&\leq\int dzdw\,\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}+\int dz\,\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\,.\end{split}

(4.51)

Thus,

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(1)}\Psi\|^{2}\leq\;&\rho^{2}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\int dzdw\,\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\,\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\rho^{2}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydz\,\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\,\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Exchanging the order of the integrals, we obtain, with Lemma
4.3
,

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(1)}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}\ell^{4}\int_{|z-w|\leq 2R}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{2}\ell^{4}\int dz\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

recalling that
\ell=C\rho^{-\delta}
and choosing
\delta>0
small enough.

Since
0\leq 1-f_{\ell}(x-y)\lesssim\chi(|x-y|\leq\ell)/|x-y|
, we immediately obtain

\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(2)}\Psi\|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2-\delta}L^{3}\,.

With (
4.39
),
(J(x)J(y)-1)^{2}\lesssim(J(x)-1)^{2}+(J(y)-1)^{2}
and exchanging
x
and
y
, we can bound (using also Lemma
4.3
)

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\|A_{x,y}^{(3)}\Psi\|^{2}&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,|k(x-y)|^{2}\|(J(x)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\int dxdydz\,|k(x-y)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3/2}\ell^{2}\int dz\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

To estimate
A^{(4)}_{x,y}
, we write
f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(y-z)-1=(f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)f_{\ell}(x-y)+(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)
and we focus on the contribution proportional to
(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)
, which we denote by
A^{(4,1)}_{x,y}
(the other contribution can be controlled similarly). We have

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(4,1)}\Psi\|^{2}&\lesssim\rho\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,k(y-z)k(y-z^{\prime})\omega_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\langle J(x)J(y)J(z^{\prime})\Psi,a_{z^{\prime}}a_{z}^{*}J(x)J(y)J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
&=\rho\int dxdydz\,|k(y-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\|J(x)J(y)J(z)\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,k(y-z)k(y-z^{\prime})\omega_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\langle a_{z}J(x)J(y)J(z^{\prime})\Psi,a_{z^{\prime}}J(x)J(y)J(z)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Estimating
0\leq J(x)\leq 1
, using the decay (
2.27
) of
k
to restrict the integrals to
|y-z|,|y-z^{\prime}|\leq R
, applying Cauchy-Schwarz and Lemma
4.1
, we find

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(4,1)}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{5/2}\ell L^{3}+\rho\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|k(y-z^{\prime})|^{2}\omega_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\|a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2-12\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

The term
A_{x,y}^{(5)}
can be handled like
A^{(4)}_{x,y}
(exchanging
x
and
y
). As for
A_{x,y}^{(6)}
, we write
J(y)J(z)-1=(J(y)-1)+J(y)(J(z)-1)
and we focus on the contribution
A^{(6,1)}_{x,y}
proportional to
(J(y)-1)
(the others can be bounded similarly). We compute

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\|A^{(6,1)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
=\;&\rho_{0}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,k(y-z)k(y-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\langle(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi,a_{z}a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\rho_{0}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydz\,|k(y-z)|^{2}\|(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\\
&+\rho_{0}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,k(y-z)k(y-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\langle a_{z^{\prime}}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi,a_{z}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

To bound the first term, we use (
4.51
). As for the second term, we apply Cauchy-Schwarz. We obtain

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A^{(6,1)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdvdw\,|k(y-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-v)\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,a_{v}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{v}\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|k(y-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho\int_{|x-y|\leq R,|y-z|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|k(y-z^{\prime})|^{2}\|a_{z}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\|^{2}+\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

where we used (
2.27
) to restrict the last integral to
|y-z|\leq R
, producing the small error
\rho^{2}L^{3}
. The first two contributions can be easily estimated, using Lemma
4.3
. In the last term, we integrate over
z^{\prime}
and we commute
a_{z}
through the operator
(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)
. We find, for
\varepsilon,\delta>0
small enough,

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\|A^{(6,1)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{5/2}\int dxdydz\,\omega_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\omega_{\ell}^{2}(y-z)\|J(x)J(y)a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&+\rho^{5/2}\int dxdydz\,\chi(|y-z|<R)\omega_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\|J(x)(J(y)-1)a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&+\rho^{5/2}\int dxdydz\,\chi(|x-z|,|y-z|<2R)\|(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\,.\end{split}

Using
0\leq\omega_{\ell}(x)\lesssim\chi(|x|\leq\ell)/|x|
,
J(x)\leq 1
and applying (
4.48
), (
4.51
), we arrive at

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\|A^{(6,1)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{5/2}\ell^{3}\int_{|w-z|\leq 2R}dwdz\,\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\rho^{5/2}\ell^{4}\int_{|w-z|,|v-z|\leq 3R}dwdvdz\,\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{v}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{v}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are chosen small enough. In the last inequality, we used Lemma
4.3
. The term
A^{(7)}_{x,y}
can be handled analogously.

To bound the term
A^{(8)}_{x,y}
, we write

\begin{split}f_{\ell}(x-w)&f_{\ell}(z-w)f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1\\
=\;&(f_{\ell}(x-w)-1)+f_{\ell}(x-w)(f_{\ell}(z-w)-1)+f_{\ell}(x-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)(f_{\ell}(x-y)-1)\\
&+f_{\ell}(x-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)f_{\ell}(x-y)(f_{\ell}(z-y)-1)\,.\end{split}

We focus on the contribution
A^{(8,1)}_{x,y}
proportional to
f_{\ell}(x-w)-1=\omega_{\ell}(x-w)
. We estimate it by

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A^{(8,1)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdwdz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}\,k(x-z)k(x-z^{\prime})k(y-w)k(y-w^{\prime})\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\omega_{\ell}(x-w^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\langle J(x)J(y)J(z)J(w)\Psi,a_{z}a_{w}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}J(x)J(y)J(z^{\prime})J(w^{\prime})\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We write

\begin{split}a_{z}a_{w}a^{*}_{w^{\prime}}a^{*}_{z^{\prime}}=\;&\delta(z-z^{\prime})\delta(w-w^{\prime})+\delta(z-w^{\prime})\delta(w-z^{\prime})+\delta(z-z^{\prime})a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w}\\
&+\delta(w-w^{\prime})a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{z}+\delta(z-w^{\prime})a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w}+\delta(w-z^{\prime})a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{z}+a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}\,.\end{split}

(4.52)

Consider first the fully contracted contribution
A^{(8,1,1)}_{x,y}
associated with
\delta(z-z^{\prime})\delta(w-w^{\prime})
. With
\|J(x)\|\leq 1
, we can bound it by

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\|A^{(8,1,1)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\leq\int dxdydzdw\,|k(x-z)|^{2}|k(y-w)|^{2}\omega^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\|\Psi\|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3}\ell L^{3}\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\delta>0
is small enough. The second fully contracted contribution can be handled similarly (using Cauchy-Schwarz). Let us now consider the contribution
A^{(8,1,7)}_{x,y}
arising from the normally ordered term
a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}
. Also here, we apply Cauchy-Schwarz. We find, using (
2.27
) to cut the integrals to
|y-w|,|y-w^{\prime}|,|x-z|,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq R
and applying Lemma
4.3
,

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&\|A^{(8,1,7)}_{x,y}\Psi\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int dxdydzdwdz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}\,|k(x-z)|^{2}|k(y-w)|^{2}\omega^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\chi(|x-y|\leq R)\chi(|y-w^{\prime}|\leq R)\chi(|x-z^{\prime}|<R)\|a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}J(x)J(y)J(z)J(w)\Psi\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{3}\ell R^{3}\int_{|z^{\prime}-w^{\prime}|\leq 3R}dz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z^{\prime}}a^{*}_{w^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}a_{z^{\prime}}\Psi\rangle\leq\rho^{2-24\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

The contribution of the other, partially contracted, terms in (
4.52
) can be handled similarly.

Finally, we consider the term
A^{(9)}_{x,y}
, on the r.h.s. of (
4.50
).
As usual, we write
(J(x)J(z)-1)=(J(x)-1)+J(x)(J(z)-1)
,
(J(y)J(w)-1)=(J(y)-1)+J(y)(J(w)-1)
and we focus, for example, on the contribution
A^{(9,1)}_{x,y}
arising from
(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)
. We find

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(9,1)}\Psi\|^{2}\leq\;&\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dwdw^{\prime}\,k(x-z)k(x-z^{\prime})k(y-w)k(y-w^{\prime})\\
&\times\langle(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi,a_{z}a_{w}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We use again (
4.52
). The fully contracted contribution
A^{(9,1,1)}_{x,y}
associated with
\delta(z-z^{\prime})\delta(w-w^{\prime})
is given by

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,&\|A_{x,y}^{(9,1,1)}\Psi\|^{2}\\
\leq\;&\int_{|x-y|<R}dxdydzdw\,|k(x-z)|^{2}|k(y-w)|^{2}\|(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{3}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydvdu\,\omega_{\ell}(x-v)\omega_{\ell}(y-u)\langle\Psi,a_{v}^{*}a_{u}^{*}a_{v}a_{u}\Psi\rangle\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{3}\ell^{4}\int_{|u-v|\leq 2R}dvdu\langle\Psi,a_{v}^{*}a_{u}^{*}a_{v}a_{u}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

The other fully contracted contribution can be bounded analogously. To bound the contribution
A^{(9,1,7)}_{x,y}
proportional to
a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}
, we apply Cauchy-Schwarz. We find, using (
2.27
) to restrict to
|x-z|,|x-z^{\prime}|,|y-w|,|y-w^{\prime}|\leq R
,

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(9,1,7)}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int_{|x-y|<R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}dwdw^{\prime}\,|k(x-z)|^{2}|k(y-w)|^{2}\\
&\hskip 22.76228pt\times\chi(|x-z^{\prime}|<R)\chi(|y-w^{\prime}|<R)\|a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{3}\int_{|x-y|<R}dxdydz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}\,\chi(|x-z^{\prime}|<R)\chi(|y-w^{\prime}|<R)\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\|a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)\Psi\|^{2}\,.\end{split}

(4.53)

We write

\begin{split}a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}(J(x)-1)&=(f_{\ell}(x-z^{\prime})f_{\ell}(x-w^{\prime})J(x)-1)a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}\\
&=(f_{\ell}(x-z^{\prime})f_{\ell}(x-w^{\prime})-1)J(x)a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}+(J(x)-1)a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}\end{split}

and we proceed similarly to pass
a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}
through
(J(y)-1)
. Let us analyze the contribution
A^{(9,1,7,1)}_{x,y}
to the integral on the r.h.s. of (
4.53
) arising from the term proportional to
(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)
; factors like
(f_{\ell}(x-z^{\prime})f_{\ell}(x-w^{\prime})-1)
force
|x-z^{\prime}|\leq\ell
or
|x-w^{\prime}|\leq\ell
and are easier to control. We have, by (
4.51
) and Lemma
4.3
,

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,\|A_{x,y}^{(9,1,7,1)}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3}\int_{|x-y|<R}dxdydz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}\,\chi(|x-z^{\prime}|<R)\chi(|y-w^{\prime}|<R)\|(J(x)-1)(J(y)-1)a_{z^{\prime}}a_{w^{\prime}}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3}\ell^{4}\int_{|z^{\prime}-w^{\prime}|,|z^{\prime}-u|,|z^{\prime}-v|<4R}dz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}dudv\langle\Psi,a_{u}^{*}a_{v}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}a_{z^{\prime}}a_{v}a_{u}\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho^{3}\ell^{4}\int_{|z^{\prime}-u|,|w^{\prime}-u|<2R}dz^{\prime}dw^{\prime}du\langle\Psi,a_{u}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}^{*}a_{w^{\prime}}a_{z^{\prime}}a_{u}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. This concludes the proof of the second bound in (
4.47
) and thus of (
4.33
).

To show (
4.34
), we expand

\begin{split}\int dx\,&\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&=\int dxdzdz^{\prime}\,\tau(x-z)\tau(x-z^{\prime})\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}_{z}c_{z^{\prime}}\Psi\rangle=\int dzdz^{\prime}(\tau*\tau)(z-z^{\prime})\langle\Psi,c^{*}_{z}c_{z^{\prime}}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

From Lemma
2.2
, we have
\|\tau*\tau\|_{1}\leq\|\tau\|_{1}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{-6\varepsilon}
and therefore, with (
4.32
),

\int dx\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{-6\varepsilon}\int dz\,\langle\Psi,c_{z}^{*}c_{z}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2-22\varepsilon-2\delta}L^{3}\,.

As for (
4.35
), we proceed similarly, writing

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdy\,&\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}(\varphi_{y})c(\varphi_{y})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&=\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,\varphi(y-z)\varphi(y-z^{\prime})\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}_{z}c_{z^{\prime}}c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Up to small errors, we can restrict the
z,z^{\prime}
integrals to
|y-z|,|y-z^{\prime}|\leq R
using (
2.27
). Hence, we obtain

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}(\varphi_{y})c(\varphi_{y})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\int_{|x-z|,|x-z^{\prime}|\leq 2R}dxdzdz^{\prime}\,(\varphi*\varphi)(z-z^{\prime})\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}_{z}c_{z^{\prime}}c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\|\varphi\|_{1}^{2}\int_{|x-z|\leq 2R}dxdz\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}_{z}c_{z}c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{-6\varepsilon}\int_{|x-z|\leq 2R}dxdz\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}_{z}c_{z}c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Repeating the same argument for
c^{*}(\tau_{x})c(\tau_{x})
, we arrive at

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|\leq R}&dxdy\,\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\tau_{x})c^{*}(\varphi_{y})c(\varphi_{y})c(\tau_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2}L^{3}+\rho^{-12\varepsilon}\int_{|w-z|\leq 3R}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,c_{z}^{*}c_{w}^{*}c_{w}c_{z}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2-42\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

by (
4.33
).
∎

Lemma
4.5
can be used to derive improved bounds on the number of particles and on the number of excitations localized in balls with size of the order
\ell\simeq\rho^{-\delta}
(which are much smaller than the balls of radius
R\simeq\rho^{-1/2-3\varepsilon}
considered in Lemma
4.3
), which will be very useful in the next section, to estimate the energy of
\Psi
.

Lemma 4.6
.

For
0<\delta<\varepsilon
,
\varepsilon>0
small enough and
\rho>0
small enough, we have

\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\langle\psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\psi\rangle,\;\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\langle\psi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{x}\psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2-36\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.

(4.54)

Moreover, recalling
R=C\rho^{-1/2-3\varepsilon}
, we find

\int_{|x-y|\leq C\ell,|x-z|\leq R}dxdydz\langle\Psi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{3/2-45\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.

(4.55)

Proof.

We start with (
4.54
). Recalling the definition (
4.2
), we can write

a_{z}^{*}a_{z}=(b_{z}^{*}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}})(b_{z}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}})\lesssim b_{z}^{*}b_{z}+\rho\,.

(4.56)

With
\ell=C\rho^{-\delta}
, we find

\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{x}\lesssim\rho^{2-3\delta}L^{3}+\rho^{1-3\delta}\int dx\,b_{x}^{*}b_{x}+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\,.

(4.57)

From Lemma
4.3
, we obtain

\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{x}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2-3\delta}L^{3}+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\,

if
\delta,\varepsilon>0
are small enough.
To control the last term, we invert (
4.31
), writing

b_{y}=c(\gamma_{y})+c^{*}(\sigma_{y})\,,\quad b_{y}^{*}=c^{*}(\gamma_{y})+c(\sigma_{y})\,.

With Cauchy-Schwarz, we find

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}&b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\\
&=\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}(c^{*}(\gamma_{y})+c(\sigma_{y}))(c(\gamma_{y})+c^{*}(\sigma_{y}))b_{x}\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c^{*}(\gamma_{y})c(\gamma_{y})b_{x}+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c(\sigma_{y})c^{*}(\sigma_{y})b_{x}\,.\end{split}

Recalling
\|\sigma_{y}\|^{2}_{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2}
, we obtain, applying Lemma
4.3
(with
k=1
),

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,&\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\sum_{\tau=\gamma,\sigma}\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})b_{x}+\rho^{3/2-3\delta}\int dx\,\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3-\varepsilon-3\delta}L^{3}+\sum_{\tau=\gamma,\sigma}\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})b_{x}\,.\end{split}

(4.58)

We have

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})b_{x}\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})c(\gamma_{x})+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,c(\sigma_{x})c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})c^{*}(\sigma_{x})\\
&\lesssim\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})c(\gamma_{x})+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\sigma_{x})c^{*}(\sigma_{x})c(\tau_{y})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,|\langle\sigma_{x},\tau_{y}\rangle|^{2}\end{split}

and therefore

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})b_{x}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})c(\gamma_{x})+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,c^{*}(\tau_{y})c^{*}(\sigma_{x})c(\sigma_{x})c(\tau_{y})\\
&\hskip 19.91684pt+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\|\sigma_{x}\|_{2}^{2}\,c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})+\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,|\langle\sigma_{x},\tau_{y}\rangle|^{2}\,.\end{split}

(4.59)

From Lemma
2.2
(the
L^{2}
-norm of
\sigma
, restricted to
|x|\leq C\ell
, can be estimated through (
2.24
)), we find

\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,|\langle\sigma_{x},\tau_{y}\rangle|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2-\delta}L^{3}

and
\|\sigma*\sigma\|_{1}\leq\|\sigma\|^{2}_{1}\lesssim\rho^{-\varepsilon}
. Hence

\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\|\sigma_{x}\|_{2}^{2}\,c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})\lesssim\rho^{3/2-3\delta}\int dy\,c^{*}(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})\lesssim\rho^{3/2-\varepsilon-3\delta}\int dz\,c^{*}_{z}c_{z}\,.

In the quartic terms on the r.h.s. of (
4.59
), we can use (
2.27
) to cutoff the kernels
\tau(z),\sigma(z),\mathbbm{1}-\gamma(z)
to
|z|\leq R
, producing only small errors. With the estimates
\|\sigma*\sigma\|_{1}\lesssim\rho^{-\varepsilon}
and
\|(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})*(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})\|_{1}\lesssim\rho^{-3\varepsilon}
, we conclude that

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,b_{x}^{*}c^{*}&(\tau_{y})c(\tau_{y})b_{x}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{-6\varepsilon}\int_{|z-w|<3R}dzdw\,c_{z}^{*}c_{w}^{*}c_{w}c_{z}+\rho^{3/2-\varepsilon-3\delta}\int dz\,c^{*}_{z}c_{z}+\rho^{2-\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Inserting in (
4.58
) and using Lemma
4.5
, we obtain

\begin{split}\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}&dxdy\,\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{-6\varepsilon}\int_{|z-w|\leq CR}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,c_{z}^{*}c_{w}^{*}c_{w}c_{z}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{3/2-\varepsilon-3\delta}\int dz\,\langle\Psi,c_{z}^{*}c_{z}\Psi\rangle+\rho^{2-\delta}L^{3}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2-36\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(4.60)

This proves the inequality (
4.54
) for the
b
-operators and, from (
4.57
), it also implies that

\int_{|x-y|<C\ell}dxdy\,\langle\Psi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{y}a_{x}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{2-36\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.

To show (
4.55
), we iterate (
4.56
) to prove that

\begin{split}&\int_{|x-y|\leq C\ell,|x-z|\leq R}dxdydz\,\langle\Psi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}a_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\lesssim\rho^{3}\ell^{3}R^{3}L^{3}+\rho^{2}\ell^{3}R^{3}\int dx\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{x}\Psi\rangle+\rho R^{3}\int_{|x-y|\leq C\ell}dxdy\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{y}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\rho\ell^{3}\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdz\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{z}^{*}b_{z}b_{x}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|x-y|\leq C\ell,|x-z|\leq R}dxdydz\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{y}^{*}b_{z}^{*}b_{z}b_{y}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Using (
4.27
) (with
k=1,2,3
) to bound the second, fourth and fifth contributions and (
4.60
) to control the third term, we obtain

\int_{|x-y|\leq C\ell,|x-z|\leq R}dxdydz\langle\Psi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}a_{x}\Psi\rangle\lesssim\rho^{3/2-45\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\,.

∎

We can also apply Lemma
4.5
to improve the rough estimate (
4.6
) on the expectation of the total number of particles
\mathcal{N}
and to show Prop.
2.4
.

Proof of Prop.
2.4
.

Recalling (
4.2
), we write

a_{x}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{x}\,,\qquad a_{x}^{*}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{x}^{*}\,.

(4.61)

We find

\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle=\rho_{0}L^{3}+2\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,\text{Re }\int dx\langle\Psi,b_{x}\Psi\rangle+\int dx\langle\Psi,b_{x}^{*}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\,.

In the last term, we apply (
4.31
). We obtain

\begin{split}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle=\;&(\rho_{0}+\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2})L^{3}+2\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,\text{Re }\int dx\langle\Psi,b_{x}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dx\,\langle\Psi,\Big[c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c(\gamma_{x})+c^{*}(\sigma_{x})c(\sigma_{x})+c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c^{*}(\sigma_{x})+c(\gamma_{x})c(\sigma_{x})\Big]\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

The error term on the first line can be bounded with Lemma
4.4
. Contributions in the second line can be estimated with Lemma
4.5
, in particular (
4.34
). For example, by Cauchy-Schwarz, we find

\begin{split}\int dx\langle&\Psi,c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c^{*}(\sigma_{x})\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq\Big[\int dx\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\gamma_{x})c(\gamma_{x})\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\Big[\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}L^{3}+\int dx\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\sigma_{x})c(\sigma_{x})\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim\rho^{7/4-11\varepsilon-\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.
∎

5
Computation of the energy

The goal of this Section is to show Proposition
2.5
. Using (
2.34
) we have

\nabla_{x}a_{x}\Psi=\Phi_{1}(x)+\Phi_{2}(x)+\Phi_{3}(x)

with

\begin{split}\Phi_{1}(x)=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,J(x)\,{d}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-\cdot)}{f_{\ell}(x-\cdot)}\bigg)\Psi\\
\Phi_{2}(x)=\;&J(x)\int dy\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\,a^{*}_{y}J(y)\Psi\\
\Phi_{3}(x)=\;&{d}\Gamma\bigg(\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-\cdot)}{f_{\ell}(x-\cdot)}\bigg)J(x)\int dy\,\nu(x-y)a^{*}_{y}J(y)\Psi,\end{split}

(5.1)

where we recall the notation
\nu=\gamma^{-1}*\sigma
, and
d\Gamma((\nabla f_{\ell}/f_{\ell})_{x})
is defined as in (
2.10
). Hence, noticing that
\Phi_{1},\Phi_{2},\Phi_{3}
are real valued,

\begin{split}&\langle\Psi,\mathcal{K}\Psi\rangle\\
&=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int\|\Phi_{j}(x)\|^{2}_{2}\,dx+2\int\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{2}(x)+\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\,dx+2\int\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\,dx\,.\end{split}

(5.2)

In the next propositions, whose proof is deferred to Subsections
5.1
-
5.6
, we evaluate the six terms on the r.h.s. of (
5.2
). Prop.
2.5
follows directly combining these estimates.

Proposition 5.1
.

We have

L^{-3}\int dx\,\|\Phi_{1}(x)\|_{2}^{2}\leq\rho_{0}(\rho_{0}+\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2})\|\nabla f_{\ell}\big\|_{2}^{2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}

(5.3)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

Proposition 5.2
.

We have

L^{-3}\int dx\,\|\Phi_{2}(x)\|_{2}^{2}\leq\big\|f_{\ell}\nabla\sigma\big\|_{2}^{2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}

(5.4)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

Proposition 5.3
.

We have

\begin{split}L^{-3}\int dx\,\|\Phi_{3}(x)\|_{2}^{2}\leq\;&\int dx\,|\sigma(x)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+\rho_{0}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

Proposition 5.4
.

We have

\begin{split}2L^{-3}\int dx\,\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{2}(x)\rangle\leq\;&2\rho_{0}\int f_{\ell}(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)\cdot\nabla\sigma(x)dx+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

(5.5)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

Proposition 5.5
.

We have

\begin{split}2L^{-3}\int dx\,\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\leq\;&2\rho_{0}\int|\nabla f(x)|^{2}\big[(\gamma\ast\sigma)(x)+(\sigma\ast\sigma)(x)\big]+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

(5.6)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

Proposition 5.6
.

We have

\begin{split}2L^{-3}\int dx\,\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\leq\;&2\int dx\,\nabla\sigma(x)\sigma(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

(5.7)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

5.1
Proof of Proposition
5.1

Writing

\Phi_{1}(x)=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dy\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)}{f_{\ell}(x-y)}J(x)\,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\Psi

and noticing that, with (
2.9
),

a_{z}J(x)^{2}a^{*}_{y}=f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)a^{*}_{y}J(x)^{2}a_{z}+\delta(z-y)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)J(x)^{2}

(5.8)

we obtain

\begin{split}\int dx\|\Phi_{1}(x)\|^{2}\leq\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdy\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\rho_{0}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Thus

\int dx\|\Phi_{1}(x)\|^{2}\leq\rho_{0}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\,\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle+C\rho_{0}\int_{|y-z|\leq C\ell}dydz\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.

Using (
2.35
) and (
4.54
), we conclude that

\int dx\|\Phi_{1}(x)\|^{2}\leq\rho_{0}(\rho_{0}+\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2})L^{3}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\big\|_{2}^{2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.
∎

5.2
Proof of Proposition
5.2

Using (
2.9
) and (
5.8
) we write

\begin{split}\int dx\,\|\Phi_{2}(x)\|_{2}^{2}=\;&\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla\nu(x-z)\big\langle\Psi,J(y)a_{y}J(x)^{2}a^{*}_{z}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\int dxdy\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)|^{2}f_{\ell}(x-y)^{2}\langle\Psi,J(y)^{2}J(x)^{2}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla\nu(x-z)f_{\ell}(x-y)^{2}f_{\ell}(x-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&\big\|f_{\ell}\nabla\nu\big\|_{2}^{2}\,L^{3}+F_{1}+F_{2}\end{split}

with

\begin{split}F_{1}=\;&\int dydz\,\big((f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)*(f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)\big)(y-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
F_{2}=\;&\int dxdydz\,(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)(x-y)(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)(x-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\,\big\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}\big(J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)-1\big)a_{y}\Psi\big\rangle.\end{split}

We analyze the two terms separately. With (
4.31
) we have

\begin{split}a_{x}=c(\gamma_{x})+c^{*}(\sigma_{x})+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,,\qquad a_{x}^{*}=c^{*}(\gamma_{x})+c(\sigma_{x})+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,.\end{split}

(5.9)

Inserting into
F_{1}
yields

\begin{split}F_{1}=F_{11}+F_{12}+F_{13}+F_{14}\end{split}

with

\begin{split}F_{11}=\;&\rho_{0}\,L^{3}\int dy\,\big((f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)*(f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)\big)(y)f_{\ell}(y)^{2}\\
F_{12}=\;&2\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dydz\,\big((f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)*(f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)\big)(y-z)f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\langle\Psi,(a_{z}-\sqrt{\rho_{0}})\Psi\rangle\\
F_{13}=\;&L^{3}\int dy\,\big((f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)*(f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)\big)(y)f_{\ell}(y)^{2}(\sigma*\sigma)(y)\\
F_{14}=\;&\int dydz\big((f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)*(f^{2}_{\ell}\nabla\nu)\big)(y-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\\
&\qquad\quad\times\big\langle\Psi,\big[c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})+c^{*}(\sigma_{y})c(\sigma_{z})+c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c^{*}(\sigma_{y})+c(\sigma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})\big]\Psi\big\rangle.\end{split}

Writing
f_{\ell}=1-\omega_{\ell}
and using
\int\nabla\nu(y)dy=0
, we find

|F_{11}|\leq C\rho L^{3}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}^{2}+C\rho L^{3}\|\omega_{\ell}\nabla\nu\|_{1}^{2}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}

(5.10)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough; here we estimated
\|\omega_{\ell}\nabla\nu\|_{1}\lesssim\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\lesssim\rho
and
\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2}
, from Lemma
2.2
. Similarly, using again Lemma
2.2
to bound
\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}\lesssim\rho^{1/2-5\varepsilon}
, we obtain

\begin{split}\Big|F_{13}-L^{3}\big(\nabla\nu&*\nabla\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(0)\Big|\\
&\leq L^{3}\int dydz\,|\nabla\nu(y-z)||\nabla\nu(z)||(\sigma*\sigma)(y)|\big(|\omega_{\ell}(y)|+|\omega_{\ell}(z)|+|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\big)\\
&\leq 3\|\sigma*\sigma\|_{\infty}\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}L^{3}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Combining (
5.10
) with (
4.28
), we immediately obtain
|F_{12}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

In order to estimate
F_{14}
, in particular the off-diagonal contribution proportional to
c(\sigma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})+c^{*}(\gamma_{y})c^{*}(\sigma_{z})
, we need to restrict the integral to
|y-z|\leq R
, with
R=\rho^{-1/2-3\varepsilon}
as in Lemma
4.1
. To this end, we are going to control the contribution from
|y-z|>R
, using the fast decay of
\nabla\nu(x)
, for
|x|\geq CR
. From Lemma
2.2
(in particular, with (
2.27
)) and keeping in mind that
\sigma(x)=0
for
|x|>C\ell_{0}
, we have

\begin{split}|\nabla\nu(x)|&=|(\gamma^{-1}*\nabla\sigma)(x)|\\
&\leq|\nabla\sigma(x)|+|((\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1})*\nabla\sigma)(x)|\\
&\lesssim\frac{\rho}{\ell}\|\nabla\sigma\|_{1}\frac{1}{(\rho^{1/2+3\varepsilon/2}|x|)^{m}}\lesssim\frac{\rho^{3/2-\varepsilon+\delta}|\log\rho|}{(\rho^{1/2+3\varepsilon/2}|x|)^{m}}\end{split}

(5.11)

for all
|x|>R/2
,
m\in\mathbb{N}
. Hence

\big|(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)*(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)(y-z)\big|\lesssim\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}\frac{\rho^{3/2-\varepsilon+\delta}|\log\rho|}{(\rho^{1/2+3\varepsilon/2}|y-z|)^{m}}\leq\frac{\rho^{2-5\varepsilon}}{(\rho^{1/2+3\varepsilon/2}|y-z|)^{m}}

for all
|y-z|>R
,
m\in\mathbb{N}
. We conclude that

\begin{split}\int_{|y-z|>R}dydz\,\big|&(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)*(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)(y-z)\big|\big|\langle\Psi,c(\sigma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\rangle\big|\\
&\lesssim\rho^{2-5\varepsilon}\int dydz\frac{\chi(|y-z|>R)}{(\rho^{1/2+3\varepsilon/2}|y-z|)^{m}}\|c^{*}(\sigma_{z})\Psi\|\|c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\|\,.\end{split}

With
\|c^{*}(\sigma_{z})\Psi\|\leq\|\sigma\|_{2}+\|c(\sigma_{z})\Psi\|
and with Cauchy-Schwarz, we can estimate

\begin{split}\int_{|y-z|>R}dydz\,\big|&(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)*(f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu)(y-z)\big|\big|\langle\Psi,c(\sigma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\rangle\big|\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{2-5\varepsilon}\int dydz\frac{\chi(|y-z|>R)}{(\rho^{1/2+3\varepsilon/2}|y-z|)^{m}}\Big[\rho^{3/2}+\|c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}+\|c(\sigma_{z})\Psi\|^{2}\Big]\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{1/2-5\varepsilon+3\varepsilon/2(m-6)}\int dy\big[\rho^{3/2}+\|c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}+\|c(\sigma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}\big]\\
\lesssim\;&\rho^{5/2-14\varepsilon+3m\varepsilon/2}L^{3}\lesssim\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small and
m\in\mathbb{N}
is large enough. In the last step, we applied (
4.34
) in Lemma
4.5
. Thus, applying Cauchy-Schwarz to handle the diagonal terms
c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})
and
c^{*}(\sigma_{y})c(\sigma_{z})
, we can bound

\begin{split}|F_{14}|\leq\;&\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}^{2}\int dx\Big[\langle\Psi,c^{*}_{x}c_{x}\rangle\Psi+\langle\Psi,c^{*}(\sigma_{x})c(\sigma_{x})\rangle\Psi+\langle\Psi,c^{*}((\gamma-\mathbbm{1})_{x})c(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})_{x})\rangle\Psi\Big]\\
&+L^{3/2}\|f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu*f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu\|_{2}\bigg(\int_{|y-z|\leq CR}\,dydz\,\|c(\gamma_{z})c(\sigma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Here we used Lemma
2.2
to estimate
\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}^{2}\leq C\rho^{1-10\varepsilon}
and
\|f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu*f_{\ell}^{2}\nabla\nu\|_{2}\leq C\|\nabla\nu\|_{4/3}^{2}\leq C\rho^{7/4-8\varepsilon}
and we applied (
4.35
). This shows that

\Big|F_{1}-\big(\nabla\nu*\nabla\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(0)L^{3}\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}

(5.12)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. To bound
F_{2}
, we apply Cauchy-Schwarz, we decompose
a_{y}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{y},a_{y}^{*}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b^{*}_{y}
as in (
4.61
) and we use

0\leq 1-J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)\leq d\Gamma(2(\omega_{\ell})_{x}+(\omega_{\ell})_{y}+(\omega_{\ell})_{z})\,.

We find

\begin{split}|F_{2}|&\leq\int dxdydz\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla\nu(x-z)|\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\Big[\rho\,\langle\Psi,(1-J^{2}(x)J(y)J(z))\Psi\rangle+\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}(1-J^{2}(x)J(y)J(z))b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]\\
&\leq\rho\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla\nu(x-z)|\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\big(|\omega_{\ell}(w-x)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-y)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-z)|\big)\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla\nu(x-z)|\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\big(|\omega_{\ell}(w-x)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-y)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-z)|\big)\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&=:F_{21}+F_{22}\,.\end{split}

With Lemma
2.2
and (
2.35
), we have

F_{21}\leq C\rho\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Writing
a_{w}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{w},a_{w}^{*}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{w}^{*}
, we obtain

\begin{split}F_{22}\leq\;&\rho\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla\nu(x-z)|\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\big(|\omega_{\ell}(w-x)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-y)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-z)|\big)\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla\nu(x-z)|\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\big(|\omega_{\ell}(w-x)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-y)|+|\omega_{\ell}(w-z)|\big)\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}b_{w}^{*}b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

In the second term, the contribution proportional to
\omega_{\ell}(y-w)
is automatically restricted to
|y-w|\lesssim\ell
. The contributions proportional to
\omega_{\ell}(w-x),\omega_{\ell}(w-z)
, on the other hand, can be restricted to
|w-y|\lesssim R
, up to a negligible error, using the fast decay (
5.11
). We find

\begin{split}F_{22}\leq\;&C\rho\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\int dy\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}b_{y}\Psi\rangle+C\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}^{2}\int_{|y-w|\leq 2\ell}dydz\,\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}b_{w}^{*}b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}\int_{|y-w|\leq R}dydz\,\langle\Psi,b_{y}^{*}b_{w}^{*}b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle+C\rho^{5/2+\varepsilon}L^{3}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\varepsilon}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough, from Lemma
2.2
, Lemma
4.3
and Lemma
4.6
.

It follows that

\int dx\,\|\Phi_{2}(x)\|_{2}^{2}\leq\big\|f_{\ell}\nabla\nu\big\|_{2}^{2}L^{3}+\big(\nabla\nu*\nabla\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(0)L^{3}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

(5.13)

To conclude the proof of the proposition, we observe that (writing
\nu=\sigma+(\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1})*\sigma
)

\begin{split}\int dx&\,(f_{\ell}^{2}(x)-1)|\nabla\nu(x)|^{2}\\
=\;&\int dx\,(f_{\ell}^{2}(x)-1)|\nabla\sigma(x)|^{2}+\int dx\,(f_{\ell}^{2}(x)-1)((\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1})*\nabla\sigma)(x)\nabla\nu(x)\\
&+\int dx\,(f_{\ell}^{2}(x)-1)\nabla\sigma(x)((\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1})*\nabla\sigma)(x)\\
\leq\;&\int dx\,(f_{\ell}^{2}(x)-1)|\nabla\sigma(x)|^{2}+C\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\|(\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1})*\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\big[\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\big]\\
\leq\;&\int dx\,(f_{\ell}^{2}(x)-1)|\nabla\sigma(x)|^{2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough (because, from Lemma
2.2
,
\|(\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1})*\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq\|\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1}\|_{2}\|\nabla\sigma\|_{2}\leq C\rho^{7/4}
,
\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty},\|\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho/\ell^{2}
). Noticing that

\begin{split}\|\nabla\nu\|^{2}_{2}&+(\nabla\nu*\nabla\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(0)\\
&=\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{k\in\Lambda_{+}^{*}}k^{2}|\widehat{\nu}_{k}|^{2}(1+|\widehat{\sigma}_{k}|^{2})=\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{k\in\Lambda_{+}^{*}}k^{2}|\widehat{\nu}_{k}|^{2}|\widehat{\gamma}_{k}|^{2}=\frac{1}{|\Lambda|}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}k^{2}|\widehat{\sigma}_{k}|^{2}=\|\nabla\sigma\|_{2}^{2}\end{split}

we arrive, from (
5.13
), at

\int dx\|\Phi_{2}(x)\|^{2}\leq\|f_{\ell}\nabla\sigma\|_{2}^{2}L^{3}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

∎

5.3
Proof of Proposition
5.3

Recall that

\Phi_{3}(x)=\int dydw\,\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)\nu(x-y)a_{w}^{*}J(x)a_{w}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi.

This implies that

\begin{split}\int dx\,\|&\Phi_{3}(x)\|^{2}\\
=\;&\int dxdydzdwdt\,\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)\cdot\nabla f_{\ell}(x-t)\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\langle a_{w}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi,J(x)a_{w}a_{t}^{*}J(x)a_{t}a_{z}^{*}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\langle a_{w}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi,J^{2}(x)a_{w}a_{z}^{*}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdydzdwdt\,\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)\cdot\nabla f_{\ell}(x-t)\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\langle a_{w}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi,J(x)a_{t}^{*}a_{w}J(x)a_{t}a_{z}^{*}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&G_{1}+G_{2}\end{split}

(5.14)

with

\begin{split}G_{1}=\;&\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\big\langle\Psi,J(y)a_{y}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a^{*}_{z}J(z)\Psi\big\rangle\\
G_{2}=\;&\int dxdydzdtdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-t)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\big\langle\Psi,J(y)a_{y}a^{*}_{t}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{t}a^{*}_{z}J(z)\Psi\big\rangle\,.\end{split}

(5.15)

Rearranging in normal order

a_{y}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}^{*}=a_{w}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{y}a_{w}+\delta(w-z)a_{z}^{*}a_{y}+\delta(w-y)a_{z}^{*}a_{y}+\delta(z-y)a_{w}^{*}a_{w}+\delta(w-y)\delta(y-z)

we can write

G_{1}\leq L^{3}\int dx\,|\nu(x)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+G_{11}+G_{12}+G_{13}

(5.16)

with

\begin{split}G_{11}=\;&\int dxdydw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{w}a_{w}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
G_{12}=\;&2\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{z}a_{y}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
G_{13}=\;&\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{y}J(z)\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

Observing with Lemma
2.1
and Lemma
2.2
that

\begin{split}\int dx\,\big|&|\nu(x)|^{2}-|\sigma(x)|^{2}\big||\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\\
\leq\;&C\big(\|\sigma\|_{\infty}+\|\nu\|_{\infty}\big)\big\|(\gamma^{-1}-\delta)*\sigma\big\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\leq C\rho\ell^{-1}\|\gamma^{-1}-\delta\|_{2}\,\|\sigma\|_{2}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

we find

G_{1}\leq L^{3}\int dx\,|\sigma(x)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+G_{11}+G_{12}+G_{13}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\,.

(5.17)

Next, we estimate the terms
G_{11},G_{12},G_{13}
. With (
2.35
), we easily find

G_{11}\leq\|\nu\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\,\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq\|\nu\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\,\rho_{0}L^{3}+C\rho^{3}L^{3}\,.

(5.18)

Switching to

G_{12}=2\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}J(z)J(y)a_{y}\Psi\rangle

we write
1-J(y)J(z)=1-J(y)+J(y)(1-J(z))
. Using the fast decay (
2.27
) of
\nu(x-z)
to restrict the integral to the region
|x-z|\leq R
producing only negligible errors, we find

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}(1-J(y))a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\Big[\int_{|x-z|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\Big[\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}.\end{split}

With Lemma
2.2
, (
4.26
) and (
4.54
), we conclude that

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}(1-J(y))a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\Big[\int_{|z-w|\leq 2R}dzdw|\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\Big[\int_{|w-y|\leq C\ell}dydw\,\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

for
\varepsilon,\delta>0
sufficiently small. The contribution proportional to
1-J(z)
can be controlled similarly. Hence

G_{12}\leq 2\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{y}\Psi\rangle+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

With
|f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)-1|\leq 2\omega_{\ell}(y-z)
and Cauchy-Schwarz, we can estimate

\begin{split}\Big|\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)&|^{2}(f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)-1)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\leq C\|\nu\|^{2}_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

for
\varepsilon,\delta>0
small enough. Therefore, using (
4.2
) to decompose
a_{y}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{y},a_{z}^{*}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}+b_{z}^{*}
and recalling that
\int\nu(x)dx=0
we arrive at

G_{12}\leq 2\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b_{z}^{*}b_{y}\Psi\rangle+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Inverting (
4.31
), we obtain

\begin{split}G_{12}\leq\;&2L^{3}\int dx\,\nu(x)(\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(x)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\\
&+2\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\Big\langle\Psi,\Big[c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})+c^{*}(\sigma_{y})c(\sigma_{z})+c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c^{*}(\sigma_{y})+c(\sigma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})\Big]\Psi\Big\rangle\\
&+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(5.19)

To bound the first term, we observe that

\nu*\sigma*\sigma=(\gamma-\mathbbm{1})*\sigma+(\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1})*\sigma

and therefore
\|\nu*\sigma*\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq(\|\gamma-\mathbbm{1}\|_{2}+\|\gamma^{-1}-\mathbbm{1}\|_{2})\|\sigma\|_{2}\leq C\rho^{3/2}
. Hence

L^{3}\int\nu(x)(\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(x)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\leq C\rho^{3/2}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\,L^{3}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

As for the second term on the r.h.s. of (
5.19
), we apply Cauchy-Schwarz together with (
4.32
), (
4.34
), (
4.35
). We obtain
G_{12}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}
.

Next, we bound
G_{13}
. With
1-J(y)J(z)\lesssim d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y}+\omega_{\ell,z})
and Cauchy-Schwarz, we find

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}(J(y)J(z)-1)a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\leq\Big[\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\times\Big[\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\,.\end{split}

(5.20)

On the one hand, writing
a_{z}^{\sharp}=b_{z}^{\sharp}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}},a_{w}^{\sharp}=b_{w}^{\sharp}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}
, we have

\begin{split}\int dxdy&dzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{2}\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\rho\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})b_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\rho\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{w}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})b_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{w}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})b_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

Making use of the fast decay (
2.27
) of the kernel
\nu
to restrict the integrals to
|x-y|,|x-z|<R
producing only negligible errors, we obtain, with (
4.27
),

\begin{split}\int dxdydzdw\,&|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{3}L^{3}+C\rho^{2}\|\nu\|_{1}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\rho\|\nu\|_{1}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\int_{|w-t|\leq R}dwdt\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{t}a_{t}b_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\|\nu\|_{\infty}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z-w|\leq R\\
|z-t|\leq R\end{subarray}}dzdwdt\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{t}a_{t}b_{z}b_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{3-c(\varepsilon+\delta)}L^{3}\end{split}

for some
c>0
. Similarly, with (
4.55
) we find

\begin{split}\int&dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\int dxdydzdwdt\,|\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-t)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{t}^{*}a_{t}a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2}L^{3}+C\|\nu\|_{1}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-t|\leq\ell\\
|y-w|\leq R\end{subarray}}dydwdt\,\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{t}^{*}a_{t}a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2-c(\varepsilon+\delta)}L^{3}\end{split}

for some
c>0
. Inserting the last two bounds in (
5.20
) we have

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}(J(y)J(z)-1)a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Hence

\begin{split}G_{13}\leq\;&\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
=\;&\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+G_{131}+G_{132}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

(5.21)

where now

\begin{split}G_{131}=\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
G_{132}=\;&2\rho_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

Using
\int\nu(x)dx=0
we find

\begin{split}G_{131}=\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\omega_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}(f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}-1)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\end{split}

and therefore

\begin{split}|G_{131}|\leq\;&C\rho\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\big[\|\nu\|_{\infty}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{2}+\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\big]\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Moreover,

\begin{split}G_{132}=\;&-2\rho_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times f_{\ell}(y-w)\omega_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+2\rho_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}(f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}-1)\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\\
&+C\rho^{1/2}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle^{1/2}\Big(\int_{|y-w|\leq CR}dydw\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

where we used the decay (
2.27
) of
\nu
to cut the integral to
|y-w|\leq CR
(producing only a negligible error), we estimated
a_{w}^{*}a_{w}\lesssim\rho+b_{w}^{*}b_{w}
and we applied Lemma
4.3
.
Plugging the last two bounds into (
5.21
) yields

\begin{split}G_{13}\leq\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+G_{133}+G_{134}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

(5.22)

with

\begin{split}G_{133}=\;&\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{w}b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
G_{134}=\;&2\rho_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

Applying Lemma
4.3
to estimate

\begin{split}|G_{133}|&\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\int_{|w-z|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{w}b_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

and

\begin{split}|G_{134}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
