&+C\rho^{1/2}\bigg(\int_{|y-w|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}b^{*}_{w}b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&\times\bigg(\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho^{1/2}\|\nu\|_{1}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Big(\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dzdw\langle\,\Psi,b_{w}^{*}b_{z}^{*}b_{z}b_{w}\Psi\rangle\Big)^{1/2}\Big(\int dz\langle\Psi,b_{z}^{*}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

we arrive at

\begin{split}G_{13}\leq\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Finally, we invert (
4.31
). We obtain

\begin{split}G_{13}\leq\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\times f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)(\sigma*\sigma)(y-z)\\
&+\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\big\langle\Psi,\big[c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})+c^{*}(\sigma_{y})c(\sigma_{z})+c^{*}(\gamma_{z})c^{*}(\sigma_{y})+c(\sigma_{y})c(\gamma_{z})\big]\Psi\big\rangle+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

We can control the second term by Cauchy-Schwarz, together with (
4.32
), (
4.34
), (
4.35
). As for the first term, we use

0\leq 1-f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)\leq C\big(\omega_{\ell}(y-z)+\omega_{\ell}(y-w)+\omega_{\ell}(z-w)\big)

to estimate

\begin{split}\big|\rho_{0}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)&\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}(\sigma*\sigma)(y-z)\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(f_{\ell}(y-z)^{2}f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(z-w)-1)\big|\\
&\leq C\rho\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\sigma*\sigma\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{1}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}L^{3}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

We conclude that

G_{13}\leq\rho_{0}L^{3}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\int dx\,\nu(x)(\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(x)+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}.

Combining this with (
5.17
), (
5.18
) and the estimate
G_{12}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, we obtain

\begin{split}G_{1}\leq\;&L^{3}\int dx\,|\sigma(x)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+\rho_{0}L^{3}\|\nu\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\\
&+\rho_{0}L^{3}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\int dx\,\nu(x)(\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(x)+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
=\;&L^{3}\int dx\,|\sigma(x)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+\rho_{0}L^{3}\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

(5.23)

where we recalled
\nu=\gamma^{-1}*\sigma
and
\sigma*\sigma=\gamma*\gamma-1
to compute
(\nu*\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(0)=(\sigma*\sigma)(0)-(\nu*\nu)(0)=\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}-\|\nu\|_{2}^{2}
.

We are left with estimating
G_{2}
from (
5.15
). Rearranging

\begin{split}a_{y}&a_{t}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{t}a_{z}^{*}\\
=\;&a_{z}^{*}a_{t}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{t}a_{y}+(\delta(t-z)+\delta(t-y))a_{z}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{y}+(\delta(w-z)+\delta(w-y))a_{z}^{*}a_{t}^{*}a_{t}a_{y}\\
&+\delta(y-z)a_{t}^{*}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{t}+(\delta(y-t)\delta(z-w)+\delta(y-w)\delta(z-t))a_{z}^{*}a_{y}\\
&+\delta(y-t)\delta(z-t)a_{w}^{*}a_{w}+\delta(y-w)\delta(z-w)a_{t}^{*}a_{t}\end{split}

we write

G_{2}=G_{21}+G_{22}+G_{23}+G_{24}+G_{25}

with

\begin{split}G_{21}=\;&\int dxdydzdtdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-t)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\big\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{t}a^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{z}a_{y}a_{w}a_{t}J(z)\Psi\big\rangle\\
G_{22}=\;&4\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\big\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{z}a_{y}a_{w}J(z)\Psi\big\rangle\\
G_{23}=\;&\int dxdydtdw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-t)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\big\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{t}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{t}J(y)\Psi\big\rangle\\
G_{24}=\;&2\int dxdydw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{w}a_{w}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
G_{25}=\;&2\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\langle\Psi,J(y)a^{*}_{z}a_{y}J(z)\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

Using (
2.27
) to cut the integral to
|z-w|\leq R
and applying (
2.9
), Cauchy-Schwarz and (
4.55
), we obtain

\begin{split}|G_{21}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
&+C\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|t-w|\leq C\ell\\
|z-w|\leq R\end{subarray}}dxdydzdtdw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-t)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{t}a^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{z}a_{z}a_{w}a_{t}\Psi\rangle\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Similarly, from (
4.26
) (with
k=2
) and (
4.54
), we find

\begin{split}|G_{22}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
&+C\|\nu\|_{\infty}\bigg(\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{z}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\times\bigg(\int_{|y-w|\leq C\ell}dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-z)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{w}a^{*}_{y}a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

We continue with

\begin{split}|G_{23}|\leq\;&C\|\nu\|_{2}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\int_{|t-w|\leq C\ell}\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{t}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{t}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

and

|G_{24}|,|G_{25}|\leq C\|\nu\|_{\infty}^{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}^{2}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}.

Hence
|G_{2}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Together with (
5.14
) and (
5.23
), this completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.4
Proof of Proposition
5.4

From (
5.1
), we find

\begin{split}2\int&dx\,\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{2}(x)\rangle\\
&=2\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\cdot\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)}{f_{\ell}(x-z)}\langle J(x)^{2}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi,a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
&=2\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\cdot\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)f_{\ell}(x-z)\langle J(x)^{2}a_{z}\Psi,a_{z}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
&=P_{1}+P_{2}\end{split}

(5.24)

with

\begin{split}P_{1}&=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\langle J(x)^{2}a_{y}\Psi,J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
P_{2}&=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle a_{y}J(x)^{2}a_{z}\Psi,a_{z}J(y)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We write

\begin{split}P_{1}=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\langle a_{y}\Psi,\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)\langle a_{y}\Psi,(J(x)^{2}J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&P_{11}+P_{12}\,.\end{split}

By Cauchy-Schwarz, we can estimate

\begin{split}|P_{12}|\leq C\sqrt{\rho}&\Big[\int dxdy|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)|\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x}+\omega_{\ell,y})a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\Big[\int dxdy|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)|\langle\Psi,d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x}+\omega_{\ell,y})\Psi\rangle\Big]^{1/2}\,.\end{split}

We have, from Prop.
2.4

\begin{split}\int dxdy\,&|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)|\langle\Psi,d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x}+\omega_{\ell,y})\Psi\rangle\\
&=2\int dxdydz\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)|\omega_{\ell}(x-z)\langle\Psi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq C\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{2-\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

and, with Lemma
4.6
,

\begin{split}\int dxdy&|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)|\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x}+\omega_{\ell,y})a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq\int dxdydz|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)|\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-z)+\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\big)\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq C\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\int_{|y-z|\leq C\ell}dydz\,\langle\Psi,a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{3-c(\varepsilon+\delta)}L^{3}\end{split}

for some
c>0
.
This implies that
|P_{12}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Moreover, with Lemma
4.4
we find

\Big|P_{11}-\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\nabla\nu(x)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x)\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Hence,

\Big|P_{1}-\rho_{0}\int dx\nabla\nu(x)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x)\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

(5.25)

On the other hand, we can write

\begin{split}P_{2}=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\big(f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)f_{\ell}(z-y)-1\big)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,(J(x)^{2}-1)J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,(J(y)-1)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&P_{21}+P_{22}+P_{23}+P_{24}\,.\end{split}

With Lemma
2.2
and Lemma
4.6
, we bound

\begin{split}|P_{21}|&\leq C\sqrt{\rho}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-y)+\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\big)\|a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\|\|a_{z}\Psi\|\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{2}\Big[\int_{|y-z|\leq C\ell}dydz\,\|a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\Big[\int dz\,\|a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Again by Lemma
2.2
and Lemma
4.6
, we get (as above, we use (
2.27
) to restrict the integral to
|x-y|<R
, with a negligible error)

\begin{split}|P_{22}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\sqrt{\rho}\Big[\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\|a_{y}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\times\Big[\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\omega_{\ell}(x-w)|\|a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\Big[\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z-w|\leq C\ell\\
|y-w|\leq R\end{subarray}}dydzdw\,\|a_{y}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\Big[\int_{|z-w|\leq C\ell}dzdw\,\|a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are sufficiently small. As for
P_{23}
, we decompose

\begin{split}P_{23}=P_{231}+P_{232}\end{split}

with

\begin{split}P_{231}=\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{y}b^{*}_{z}(J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\\
P_{232}=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{y}a^{*}_{z}(J(y)-1)b_{z}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

With Cauchy-Schwarz,
(1-J(y))^{2}\leq 1-J(y)\lesssim d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,y})
and Lemma
4.3
, we find

\begin{split}|P_{231}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{y}a_{y}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

and, also with Lemma
4.6
,

\begin{split}|P_{232}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho^{1/2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\bigg(\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\bigg(\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-w|\leq C\ell\\
|y-z|\leq R\end{subarray}}dydzdw\,\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{y}a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{z}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.
Thus,
|P_{23}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
. Finally, since
\int\nabla\nu(x)dx=\int\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x)dx=0
, we can write
P_{24}=P_{241}+P_{242}
, with

\begin{split}P_{241}=\;&\rho_{0}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle b_{y}b_{z}\Psi,\Psi\rangle\\
P_{242}=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\langle b_{y}a_{z}\Psi,b_{z}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

With Lemma
4.3
, we estimate

\begin{split}|P_{241}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho\,\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}L^{3/2}\bigg(\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{x}b^{*}_{y}b_{y}b_{x}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

(5.26)

and

\begin{split}|P_{242}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
&+C\rho^{1/2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\bigg(\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}a^{*}_{z}a_{z}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\int dz\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho^{1/2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\bigg(\int dz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\bigg(\rho R^{3}\int dy\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}b_{y}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}b^{*}_{z}b_{z}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. This shows that
|P_{2}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are sufficiently small and, together with (
5.24
and (
5.25
), it shows that

2\int dx\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{2}(x)\rangle\leq 2\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,f_{\ell}(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)\nabla\nu(x)+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Since, from Lemma
2.2
,

\begin{split}\rho_{0}\Big|\int dxf_{\ell}(x)&\nabla f_{\ell}(x)\big((\gamma^{-1}-1)*\nabla\sigma\big)(x)\Big|\\
&\leq C\rho\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|(\gamma^{-1}-1)*\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho\ell\|\gamma^{-1}-1\|_{2}\|\nabla\sigma\|_{2}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough, this concludes the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.5
Proof of Proposition
5.5

From (
5.1
), we have

\begin{split}\int&dx\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\\
=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)}{f_{\ell}(x-z)}\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)}{f_{\ell}(x-w)}\langle J(x)^{2}a_{z}^{*}a_{z}\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\langle J(x)^{2}a_{z}\Psi,a_{z}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}{4}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle J(x)^{2}a_{w}a_{z}\Psi,a_{w}a_{z}a_{y}^{*}J(y)\Psi\rangle\end{split}

where we used the identity (
2.9
) to pass
a_{z}^{*},a_{w}
through
J(x)^{2}
. Commuting also
a_{y}^{*}
to the left, we arrive at

\begin{split}\int&dx\langle\Phi_{1}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\\
=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\langle a_{y}\Psi,J^{2}(x)J(y)\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(y-z)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J^{2}(x)J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}{2}\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)f_{\ell}(y-z)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J^{2}(x)J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{\sqrt{\rho_{0}}}{4}\int dxdydzdw\,\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-z)\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-w)f_{\ell}^{2}(x-y)f_{\ell}(y-w)f_{\ell}(y-z)\ \\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\langle a_{w}a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J^{2}(x)J(y)a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
=:\;&Q_{1}+Q_{2}+Q_{3}+Q_{4}\,.\end{split}

(5.27)

The term
Q_{4}
is negligible. In fact, with Lemma
4.6
and using (
2.27
) to handle the region
|w-y|>R
, we find

\begin{split}|Q_{4}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\sqrt{\rho}\Big[\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|w-z|\leq C\ell,\\
|w-y|\leq R\end{subarray}}dxdydzdw|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\|a_{y}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\times\Big[\int_{|w-z|\leq C\ell}dxdydzdw|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\|a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\Big[\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|w-z|\leq C\ell,\\
|w-y|\leq R\end{subarray}}dydzdw\,\|a_{y}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\Big[\int_{|w-z|\leq C\ell}dwdz\,\|a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

(5.28)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Also
Q_{3}
is negligible, since

\begin{split}|Q_{3}|\leq\;&C\sqrt{\rho}\int dxdydz\,|\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|\|a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\|\|a_{z}\Psi\|\\
\leq\;&C\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}\Big[\int_{|y-z|\leq C\ell}dydz\,\|a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\Big[\int dz\|a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(5.29)

As for the term
Q_{1}
, we decompose it as

\begin{split}Q_{1}=\;&\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,\nu(x)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dx\,\nu(x)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\int dy\langle b_{y}\Psi,\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdy\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\langle a_{y}\Psi,(J^{2}(x)J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,\nu(x)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}+Q_{12}+Q_{13}\,.\end{split}

From Lemma
4.4
, we have

|Q_{12}|\leq C\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\Big|\int dy\,\langle b_{y}\Psi,\Psi\rangle\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

With
0\leq 1-J^{2}(x)J(y)\lesssim d\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x}+\omega_{\ell,y})
, we find

\begin{split}|Q_{13}|\leq\;&C\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\Big[\int dxdydw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-w)+\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\big)\|a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big[\int dxdydw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-w)+\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\big)\|a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\>&C\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|^{2}_{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\Big[\int_{|y-w|\leq C\ell}dydw\,\|a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\Big[\int dw\,\|a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(5.30)

We conclude that

\Big|Q_{1}-\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,\nu(x)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}

(5.31)

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Finally, we consider
Q_{2}
. Proceeding as in (
5.30
), we find

\begin{split}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,\Big|&\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\big(f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(y-z)-1\big)\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J^{2}(x)J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-y)+\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\big)\|a_{y}a_{z}\Psi\|\|a_{z}\Psi\|\\
&\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Moreover, we have

\begin{split}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\,&\Big|\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,(J^{2}(x)-1)J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\sqrt{\rho}\Big[\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\|a_{y}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\Big[\int dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\|a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
&+C\sqrt{\rho}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\Big[\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-z|\leq CR,\\
|w-z|\leq C\ell\end{subarray}}dydzdw\,\|a_{y}a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big[\int_{|z-w|\leq C\ell}dzdw\|a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Thus,

\Big|Q_{2}-\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Using (
4.61
) and
\int\nu(x)dx=0
, we can further argue that

\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,\nu(x-y)|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\langle a_{y}a_{z}\Psi,J(y)a_{z}\Psi\rangle=Q_{21}+Q_{22}+Q_{23}+Q_{24}

(5.32)

with

Q_{21}=\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{y}a_{z}\Psi\rangle,

(5.33)

and

\begin{split}Q_{22}=\;&{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{y}(J(y)-1)b_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
Q_{23}=\;&{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{y}(J(y)-1)\Psi\rangle\\
Q_{24}=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{y}(J(y)-1)b_{z}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We claim that
Q_{22},Q_{23},Q_{24}
are negligible. In fact,

\begin{split}|Q_{22}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho\bigg(\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}|\nu(x-y)|^{2}\langle\Psi,a^{*}_{y}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\times\bigg(\int_{|x-y|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle^{1/2}\\
&\qquad\times\bigg(\rho R^{3}\int dz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{z}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|z-w|\leq CR}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{w}b_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

and, analogously,
|Q_{23}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.
Moreover, by (
4.61
) and (
4.27
)

\begin{split}|Q_{24}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
&+\rho^{1/2}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\bigg(\int_{|y-x|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{y}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}a_{y}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\times\bigg(\int_{|y-x|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{w}a_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho^{1/2}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\,\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\\
&\quad\times\bigg(\rho R^{3}\int_{|y-w|\leq\ell}dydw\,\|a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-w|\leq\ell\\
|z-y|\leq CR\end{subarray}}dydzdw\,\|a_{z}a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&\quad\times\bigg(\rho R^{3}\int dz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{z}\Psi\rangle+\int_{|z-w|\leq CR}dzdw\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{w}b_{w}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. We conclude that
|Q_{2}-Q_{21}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
, with
Q_{21}
defined as in (
5.33
). Using again
\int\nu(x)dx=0
, we write

Q_{21}=Q_{211}+Q_{212}+Q_{213}

with

\begin{split}Q_{211}=\;&{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
Q_{212}=\;&{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
Q_{213}=\;&\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\int dxdydz\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|^{2}\nu(x-y)\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b^{*}_{y}b_{z}\Psi\rangle.\end{split}

(5.34)

The last term satisfies

\begin{split}|Q_{213}|\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
&+C\rho^{1/2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\bigg(\int dz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{z}b_{z}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\,\langle\Psi,b^{*}_{y}b^{*}_{z}b_{z}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

which follows from (
4.7
) and (
4.27
). For
Q_{211}
we recall from (
4.31
) that
b_{x}^{*}=c^{*}(\gamma_{x})+c(\sigma_{x})
for
x=z,w
. Using
\gamma*\nu=\sigma
, (
2.27
) to restrict the integral to
|z-y|\leq R
and Lemma
4.5
, we find

\begin{split}\bigg|Q_{211}-&\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}(\sigma*\sigma)(x)\bigg|\\
\leq\;&C\rho L^{3/2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\bigg(\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\,\Big(\|c(\gamma_{z})c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}+\|c(\sigma_{z})c(\sigma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}\Big)\bigg)^{1/2}\\
&+C\rho\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\nu\|_{1}\bigg(\int dz\,\|c(\gamma_{z})\Psi\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\int dz\,\|c(\sigma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Similarly,

\begin{split}\bigg|Q_{212}-&\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\big(\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(x)\bigg|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\rho\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\nu\|_{1}\int dy\,\Big(\|c(\gamma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}+\|c(\sigma_{y})\Psi\|^{2}\Big)\\
&+C\rho L^{3/2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\bigg(\int_{|y-z|\leq R}dydz\,\big\|c(\gamma_{y})c(\sigma_{z})\Psi\big\|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Thus,

\begin{split}\Big|Q_{2}-&\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}(\sigma*\sigma)(x)-\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\big(\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(x)\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Together with (
5.27
), (
5.28
), (
5.29
), (
5.31
) and with the observation that

\begin{split}\rho_{0}L^{3}\int&dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\nu(x)+\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}\big(\nu*\sigma*\sigma\big)(x)\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt=\rho_{0}L^{3}\int dx\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x)|^{2}(\gamma*\sigma)(x)\end{split}

this completes the proof of the proposition.
∎

5.6
Proof of Proposition
5.6

From (
5.1
), we have

\begin{split}\int dx&\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\\
=\;&\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)}{f_{\ell}(x-w)}\langle J(y)\Psi,a_{y}a_{w}^{*}a_{w}J(x)^{2}a_{z}^{*}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)}{f_{\ell}(x-y)}\langle J(y)\Psi,a_{y}J(x)^{2}a_{z}^{*}J(z)\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\frac{\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)}{f_{\ell}(x-w)}\langle J(y)\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{w}a_{y}J(x)^{2}a_{z}^{*}J(z)\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

Using again the identity (
2.9
) and the canonical commutation relations to move the operator
a_{z}^{*}
to the left, we obtain

\begin{split}&\int dx\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\\
&=\int dxdy\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y)\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)f_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)^{2}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-y)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)^{2}a_{z}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\langle a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times f_{\ell}(w-y)f_{\ell}(w-z)\langle a_{w}a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&=:R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5}\,.\end{split}

With
1-J(x)^{2}J(y)^{2}\leq Cd\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x}+\omega_{\ell,y})
and with the usual estimates from Lemma
2.2
and Prop.
2.4
, we can bound

\begin{split}\Big|&R_{1}-L^{3}\int dx\nabla\nu(x)\nu(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)\Big|\\
&\leq\int dxdydw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-w)+\omega_{\ell}(y-w)\big)\langle\Psi,a_{w}^{*}a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&\leq C\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

(5.35)

To estimate
R_{2}
, we observe that

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)\big(f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)f^{2}_{\ell}(y-z)-1\big)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\langle a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&C\int dxdydz|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-z)+\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\big)\|a_{z}\Psi\|\|a_{y}\Psi\|\\
\leq\;&C\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Decomposing now

J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)-1=(J(x)^{2}-1)J(y)J(z)+(J(y)-1)J(z)+(J(z)-1)

and focusing for example on the contribution proportional to
1-J(x)^{2}\leq Cd\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,x})
, we can bound, by Cauchy-Schwarz and applying (
2.27
) to estimate the contribution from
|z-w|>R
,

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)\langle a_{z}\Psi,(J(x)^{2}-1)J(y)J(z)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\Big[\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dxdydz|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-y)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\|a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\Big[\int_{|y-w|\leq C\ell}dxdydz|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-w)\|a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\Big[\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dzdw\,\|a_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\Big[\int_{|y-w|\leq C\ell}dydw\,\|a_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough, as it follows from (
4.26
) and (
4.54
). The contributions proportional to
J(y)-1
and
J(z)-1
can be bounded similarly. Recalling that
\int\nu(x)dx=0
, we obtain

\Big|R_{2}-\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)\langle\Psi,b_{z}^{*}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Recalling (
4.31
), we write now
b^{*}_{z}=c^{*}(\gamma_{z})+c(\sigma_{z})
and similarly for
b_{y}
. With Lemma
4.5
, we can control the resulting terms, except the commutator
[c(\sigma_{z}),c^{*}(\sigma_{y})]=(\sigma*\sigma)(z-y)
, similarly as we did for the term
Q_{211}
in (
5.34
). We conclude that

\Big|R_{2}-L^{3}\int dx\,\nabla\nu(x)(\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

(5.36)

The term
R_{3}
is negligible, since, by Prop.
2.4
,

\begin{split}|R_{3}|&\leq C\int dxdydz\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\|a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\leq\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough.

Proceeding as we did above for
R_{2}
, we can show that

\Big|R_{4}-\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydz\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)\langle\Psi,b_{z}^{*}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Since

\begin{split}\Big|\int dxdydz\,&\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)\langle\Psi,b_{z}^{*}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\leq\;\|\nu\|_{\infty}\int dxdydz|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-z)|\|b_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&\leq\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla\nu\|_{1}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\int dx\|b_{x}\Psi\|^{2}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough, we conclude that
|R_{4}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
.

Finally, we estimate
R_{5}
. Similarly as in the analysis of
R_{2},R_{4}
, we first control the contribution of the difference

\begin{split}1-f^{2}_{\ell}(x-y)f^{2}_{\ell}(x-z)&f^{2}_{\ell}(z-y)f_{\ell}(w-y)f_{\ell}(w-z)\\
&\leq C\big(\omega_{\ell}(x-y)+\omega_{\ell}(x-z)+\omega_{\ell}(z-y)+\omega_{\ell}(w-y)+\omega_{\ell}(w-z)\big)\,.\end{split}

Focussing for example on the term proportional to
\omega_{\ell}(x-y)
, its contribution to
R_{5}
can be estimated by

\begin{split}\int&dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\|a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\|\|a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\Big[\int_{|w-z|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\|a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Big[\int_{|w-y|\leq C\ell}dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-z)|^{2}\omega_{\ell}(x-y)\|a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\|\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\Big[\int_{|w-z|\leq R}dzdw\,\|a_{w}a_{z}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\Big[\int_{|w-y|\leq C\ell}dydw\,\|a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Most of the other contributions can be bounded similarly. Only the term proportional to
\omega_{\ell}(y-z)
requires a different procedure. In this case, we decompose

\begin{split}\int&dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\langle a_{w}a_{z}\Psi,a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle|\\
\leq\;&\rho\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)\|a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\\
&+\sqrt{\rho}\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\langle a_{w}\Psi,a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho}\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\langle a_{w}b_{z}\Psi,a_{w}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)||\nu(x-z)||\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\langle a_{w}b_{z}\Psi,a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle|\\
=\;&R_{51}+R_{52}+R_{53}+R_{54}\,.\end{split}

By Cauchy-Schwarz, we have

R_{51}\leq\rho\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Moreover,

\begin{split}R_{52}\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}+\sqrt{\rho}\,\|\nu\|_{\infty}\Big[\int_{|w-y|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\|a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\times\Big[\int dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(y-z)|\|a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\sqrt{\rho}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\langle\Psi,\mathcal{N}\Psi\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\Big[\rho R^{3}\int dw\|b_{w}\Psi\|^{2}+\int_{|w-y|\leq R}dwdy\,\|b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

and similarly for
R_{53}
. As for
R_{54}
, we use Lemma
4.3
to estimate

\begin{split}R_{54}\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\int_{|w-z|\leq R}dwdz\,\|b_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\|\nabla\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\Big[\rho R^{3}\int dz\|b_{z}\Psi\|^{2}+\int_{|w-z|\leq R}dwdz\,\|b_{z}b_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/3+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Thus,

\begin{split}\Big|R_{5}-\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{w}a_{z}\Psi,J(x)^{2}&J(y)J(z)a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
&\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

Next, we write
1-J(x)^{2}J(y)J(z)=(1-J(x)^{2})J(y)J(z)+(J(y)-1)J(z)+J(z)-1
. We focus on the contribution proportional to
1-J(z)\leq Cd\Gamma(\omega_{\ell,z})
. Observing that
\int\nabla f_{\ell}^{2}(x-w)dw=0
, we find

\begin{split}\int dxdy&dzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{w}a_{z}\Psi,(J(z)-1)a_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
=\;&\sqrt{\rho}\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{z}\Psi,(J(z)-1)b_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\rho}\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{z}b_{w}\Psi,(J(z)-1)a_{y}\Psi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{z}b_{w}\Psi,(J(z)-1)b_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\,.\end{split}

We estimate the last term; the other two can be bounded similarly. With Cauchy-Schwarz, we have

\begin{split}&\Big|\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{z}b_{w}\Psi,(J(z)-1)b_{w}a_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\|\nu\|_{\infty}\Big[\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z-t|\leq C\ell,\\
|z-w|\leq R\end{subarray}}dxdydzdwdt\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\|a_{z}b_{w}a_{t}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\Big[\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-w|\leq R,\\
|y-t|\leq R\end{subarray}}dxdydzdwdt\,|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\omega_{\ell}(z-t)\|a_{y}b_{w}a_{t}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+C\|\nu\|_{\infty}\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{1}^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\Big[\rho R^{3}\int_{|z-t|\leq C\ell}dzdt\|a_{z}a_{t}\Psi\|^{2}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|z-t|\leq C\ell,\\
|z-w|\leq R\end{subarray}}dzdtdw\|a_{z}a_{t}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big[\rho^{2}R^{6}\int dw\|b_{w}\Psi\|^{2}+\rho R^{3}\int_{|y-w|\leq R}\hskip-14.22636ptdwdy\|b_{w}b_{y}\Psi\|^{2}+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}|y-w|\leq R,\\
|y-t|\leq R\end{subarray}}dwdydt\|b_{w}b_{y}b_{t}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.\end{split}

The contributions proportional to
1-J(y)
and
1-J(x)^{2}
can be treated similarly. Recalling that
\int\nu(x)dx=\int\nabla\nu(x)dx=0
, we obtain

\Big|R_{5}-\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{w}b_{z}\Psi,a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Estimating

\begin{split}\Big|\int dx&dydzdw\,\nabla\nu(x-y)\nu(x-z)\nabla f^{2}_{\ell}(x-w)\langle a_{w}b_{z}\Psi,a_{w}b_{y}\Psi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\Big[\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nabla\nu(x-y)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\|b_{z}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Big[\int_{|y-w|\leq R}dxdydzdw\,|\nu(x-z)|^{2}|\nabla f_{\ell}(x-w)|\|b_{y}a_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}+\|\nabla\nu\|_{2}\|\nu\|_{2}\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\Big[\rho R^{3}\int dz\,\|b_{z}\Psi\|^{2}+\int_{|z-w|\leq R}dzdw\,\|b_{z}b_{w}\Psi\|^{2}\Big]\\
\leq\;&C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\end{split}

we conclude that
|R_{5}|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}
.

Summarizing, we proved that

\Big|\int dx\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle-L^{3}\int dx\nabla\nu(x)\big[\nu(x)+(\nu*\sigma*\sigma)(x)\big]\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)\Big|\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

Recalling that
\nu=\gamma^{-1}*\sigma
and that
\sigma*\sigma=\gamma*\gamma-1
, we find
\nu+\nu*\sigma*\sigma=\gamma*\sigma
. Hence,

2\int dx\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\leq 2L^{3}\int dx\,\nabla\nu(x)(\gamma*\sigma)(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}\,.

(5.37)

Next, we observe that

\begin{split}\Big|\int dx\,((\gamma^{-1}-1)*\nabla\sigma)&(x)(\gamma*\sigma)(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)\Big|\\
&\leq\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|(\gamma^{-1}-1)*\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\|\gamma*\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\end{split}

if
\varepsilon,\delta>0
are small enough. Here, we used Lemma
2.2
to estimate
\|(\gamma^{-1}-1)*\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq\|\gamma^{-1}-1\|_{2}\|\nabla\sigma\|_{2}\leq C\rho^{7/4}
and
\|\gamma*\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq\|\gamma-1\|_{2}\|\sigma\|_{2}+\|\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho
. Moreover, again by Lemma
2.2
, we have

\begin{split}\Big|\int dx\,\nabla\sigma(x)((\gamma-1)*\sigma)(x)&\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)\Big|\\
&\leq\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{1}\|\nabla\sigma\|_{\infty}\|\gamma-\mathbbm{1}\|_{2}\|\sigma\|_{2}\leq C\rho^{5/2}\ell^{-1}\leq C\rho^{5/2+\delta}\,.\end{split}

Hence, we conclude from (
5.37
) that

2\int dx\langle\Phi_{2}(x),\Phi_{3}(x)\rangle\leq 2L^{3}\int dx\,\nabla\sigma(x)\sigma(x)\nabla f_{\ell}(x)f_{\ell}(x)+C\rho^{5/2+\delta}L^{3}.

∎

Appendix A
Proof of Lemma
2.2

Proof.

Given a sequence
\widehat{f}=\{\widehat{f}_{k}\}_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}
, on the dual lattice
\Lambda^{*}=2\pi\mathbb{Z}^{3}/L
, we define its discrete derivative
\delta_{j}\widehat{f}
in the
e_{j}
direction (for
j=1,2,3
), setting

(\delta_{j}\widehat{f})_{k}:=\frac{L}{2\pi}\big(\widehat{f}_{k+\frac{2\pi}{L}e_{j}}-\widehat{f}_{k}\big)\,.

Here
\{e_{1},e_{2},e_{3}\}
is the canonical basis of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

From the elementary inequality
|e^{-iy}-1|/|y|\geq 2/\pi
, for all
y\in[-\pi,\pi]
, we obtain the bound

|x_{j}^{m}f(x)|\leq\Big(\frac{\pi}{2}\Big)^{m}\Big|\Big(\frac{L}{2\pi}\Big)^{m}\big(e^{-i\frac{2\pi}{L}x_{j}}-1\big)^{m}f(x)\Big|=\Big(\frac{\pi}{2}\Big)^{m}\big|(\widecheck{\delta_{j}\widehat{f}})(x)\big|

(A.1)

which will be repeatedly used in this proof. Here

(\widecheck{\delta_{j}\widehat{f}})(x)=\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}(\delta_{j}\widehat{f})_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}

denotes the periodic function on
\Lambda
, with Fourier coefficients
\delta_{j}\widehat{f}
.

We are going to apply (
A.1
) to estimate the kernels
\tilde{\sigma},\sigma,\gamma-\mathbbm{1},\gamma^{-1}*\sigma
in position space. We consider first the kernel
s
, defined by the Fourier coefficients (
2.18
). To bound the discrete derivatives of
\widehat{s}_{k}
, it is convenient to define for
k\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus\{0\}

\widehat{s}(k)=-\frac{k^{2}+\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)-\sqrt{|k|^{4}+2k^{2}\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}}{\sqrt{\rho_{0}^{2}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)^{2}-\Big(k^{2}+\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)-\sqrt{|k|^{4}+2k^{2}\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}\Big)^{2}}}

with
\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)=8\pi\sin(|k|\mathfrak{a})/|k|
and
\widehat{s}(0):=0
, as a function on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, and to control its regular derivatives. For
m\in\mathbb{N}
, we find

\displaystyle\left|\nabla^{m}\widehat{s}(k)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho^{\frac{1}{4}}\,|k|^{-m-\frac{1}{2}}\qquad\hskip 28.45274pt\text{for $|k|\leq\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}$},

(A.2)

\displaystyle\left|\nabla^{m}\widehat{s}(k)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho\,|k|^{-m-2}\qquad\hskip 36.98866pt\text{for $\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq 1$}\,,

(A.3)

\displaystyle\left|\nabla^{m}\widehat{s}(k)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho\,|k|^{-3}\qquad\hskip 51.21504pt\text{for $|k|\geq 1$}\,.

(A.4)

which in particular imply the bound (
2.22
), choosing
m=0
. To prove (
A.2
), we write
\widehat{s}(k)=(k^{2}/\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k))^{-1/4}F_{1}\big(\sqrt{k^{2}/\rho_{0}V_{\text{eff}}(k)}\big)
, where

F_{1}(x)=-\frac{x^{2}+1-x\sqrt{x^{2}+2}}{\sqrt{2(x^{2}+1)\sqrt{x^{2}+2}-2x^{3}-4x}}

is a smooth function on
\mathbb{R}
. Together with the bound

\Big|\nabla^{n}\Big(\frac{k^{2}}{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}\Big)^{\alpha}\Big|\lesssim\rho_{0}^{-\alpha}|k|^{2\alpha-n}

valid for
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
, and with the chain rule, we arrive at (
A.2
). As for (
A.3
) and (
A.4
), we write
\widehat{s}(k)=(\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)/k^{2})F_{2}(\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)/k^{2})
, with

F_{2}(x)=-\frac{\frac{1+x-\sqrt{1+2x}}{x^{2}}}{\sqrt{1-x^{2}\Big(\frac{1+x-\sqrt{1+2x}}{x^{2}}\Big)^{2}}}\,.

Since
x\to(1+x-\sqrt{1+2x})/x^{2}
, is analytic in a neighborhood of
x=0
, (
A.3
), (
A.4
) follow from the bounds

\Big|\nabla^{n}\Big(\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}{k^{2}}\Big)\Big|\lesssim\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\rho|k|^{-2-n}&\text{if $\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq 1$}\\
\rho|k|^{-3}&\text{if $|k|\geq 1$}\end{array}\right.

(A.5)

and from the chain rule.

For
|k|\geq 1
, the estimate (
A.4
) can be improved significantly by subtracting the leading order term. We find

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla^{m}\Big[\widehat{s}(k)+\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)}{2|k|^{2}}\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\rho^{2}}{|k|^{6}},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \textrm{ for $|k|\geq 1$}.

(A.6)

Since the slow decay in
k
on the r.h.s. of (
A.5
) (in the regime
|k|\geq 1
) is due to the lack of decay of the derivatives of
\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}
, we can also improve (
A.4
) dividing
\widehat{s}(k)
by
\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)
. We obtain

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla^{m}\Big[\frac{\widehat{s}(k)}{\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)}\Big]\Big|\leq\Big|\nabla^{m}\Big[\frac{\widehat{s}(k)}{\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)}+\frac{\rho_{0}}{2|k|^{2}}\Big]\Big|+\Big|\nabla^{m}\Big[\frac{\rho_{0}}{2|k|^{2}}\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\rho^{2}}{|k|^{5}}+\frac{\rho}{|k|^{2+m}}

(A.7)

for all
|k|\geq 1
. Writing

(\delta_{j}\widehat{s})_{k}=\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\,(\nabla_{j}\widehat{s})\,\big(k+2\pi\tau e_{j}/L\big)

and similarly for the higher derivatives, we conclude that the bounds (
A.2
), (
A.4
), (
A.6
), (
A.7
) also translate into similar estimates for the discrete derivatives of the Fourier coefficients
\widehat{s}_{k}
. We obtain, for all
j=1,2,3
,
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

\begin{split}\big|(\delta_{j}^{m}\widehat{s})_{k}\big|&\lesssim\rho^{\frac{1}{4}}\,|k|^{-m-\frac{1}{2}}\hskip 28.45274pt\text{if $|k|\leq\rho^{1/2}$}\\
\big|(\delta_{j}^{m}\widehat{s})_{k}\big|&\lesssim\rho\,|k|^{-m-2}\hskip 36.98866pt\text{if $\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq 1$}\\
\big|(\delta_{j}^{m}\widehat{s})_{k}\big|&\lesssim\rho\,|k|^{-3}\hskip 51.21504pt\text{if $|k|\geq 1$}\end{split}

(A.8)

and also

\begin{split}\Big|\delta_{j}^{m}\Big[\widehat{s}_{k}+\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}{2k^{2}}\Big]\Big|\lesssim\frac{\rho^{2}}{|k|^{6}},\qquad\Big|\delta_{j}^{m}\Big[\frac{\widehat{s}_{k}}{\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}\Big]\Big|\leq\frac{\rho^{2}}{|k|^{5}}+\frac{\rho}{|k|^{2+m}}\end{split}

(A.9)

if
|k|\geq 1
.

Using the estimates (
A.8
), (
A.9
), we now show the first pointwise bound in (
2.23
). To this end, we first claim that

\displaystyle\left|{s}(x)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\rho}{|x|}\frac{1}{(\rho^{1/2}|x|)^{3/2}}

(A.10)

for all
|x|\geq\rho^{-1/2}
. To prove (
A.10
), we choose
\chi\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
with
\chi(z)=1
, for
|z|\leq 2
and
\chi(z)=0
for
|z|\geq 4
and, for
\mu>0
, we define
\chi_{\mu}(z)=\chi(z/\mu)
. Then, we decompose

\begin{split}s(x)=\;&\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\chi_{|x|^{-1}}(k)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}+\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\big(\chi(k)-\chi_{|x|^{-1}}(k)\big)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}\\
&+\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\big(1-\chi(k)\big)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}\\
=\;&A(x)+B(x)+C(x)\,.\end{split}

(A.11)

The first estimate in (
A.8
), with
m=0
, implies that

|A(x)|\leq\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{|k|\leq 2|x|^{-1}}|\widehat{s}_{k}|\lesssim\frac{\rho^{1/4}}{L^{3}}\sum_{|k|\leq 2|x|^{-1}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{|k|}}\lesssim\frac{\rho^{1/4}}{|x|^{5/2}}\,.

Moreover, combining (
A.8
) with the observation
(\delta\chi)_{k}=0
, if
|k|<1
or
|k|>5
, we find (keeping in mind the assumption
|x|\geq\rho^{-1/2}
)

\big||x|^{3}B(x)\big|\lesssim\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\Big|\delta_{j}^{3}\big((\chi-\chi_{|x|^{-1}})\widehat{s}\big)_{k}\Big|\lesssim\rho^{1/4}|x|^{1/2}\,.

Hence
|B(x)|\lesssim\rho^{1/4}/|x|^{5/2}
. To bound
C(x)
, we define
\widehat{Q}_{k}:=\big(1-\chi_{1}(k)\big)\widehat{s}(k)/\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)
, for
k\in\Lambda^{*}
, and we denote by
Q
the periodic function with Fourier coefficients
\widehat{Q}_{k}
. We have, recalling the normalization (
3.24
) for norms in Fourier space,

\displaystyle\big|C(x)\big|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L^{3}}\Big|\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}e^{ik\cdot x}\widehat{Q}(k)\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)\Big|=\big|(V_{\mathrm{eff}}*Q)(x)\big|\lesssim\Big|\int_{\partial B_{\mathfrak{a}}(x)}Q(y)dy\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim|x|^{-3}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sup_{y}\big|y_{j}^{3}Q(y)\big|\lesssim|x|^{-3}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\big\|\delta_{j}^{3}\widehat{Q}\big\|_{1}\lesssim\rho|x|^{-3}

(A.12)

where we first used that
|x|\leq 2|y|
for
y\in\partial B_{\mathfrak{a}}(x)
and
|x|\geq 2\mathfrak{a}
, and subsequently (
A.9
) to estimate the discrete derivatives. We obtain that
|C(x)|\lesssim\rho|x|^{-3}\leq\rho^{1/4}|x|^{-5/2}
for all
|x|\geq\rho^{-1/2}
, which concludes the proof of (
A.10
). For
2\mathfrak{a}\leq|x|\leq\rho^{-1/2}
, we show that

|s(x)|\lesssim\frac{\rho}{|x|}\,.

(A.13)

To this end, we proceed analogously to (
A.11
), decomposing

\begin{split}s(x)=\;&\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\chi_{\rho^{1/2}}(k)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}+\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\big(\chi_{|x|^{-1}}(k)-\chi_{\rho^{1/2}}(k)\big)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}\\
&+\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\big(\chi(k)-\chi_{|x|^{-1}}(k)\big)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}+\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\big(1-\chi(k)\big)\widehat{s}_{k}e^{ik\cdot x}\\
=\;&\tilde{A}_{1}(x)+\tilde{A}_{2}(x)+B(x)+C(x)\,.\end{split}

With (
A.8
), we estimate

||x|\tilde{A}_{1}(x)|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\delta_{j}\big(\chi_{\rho^{1/2}}\widehat{s}\big)\|_{1}\lesssim\rho\,.

Similarly,

|\tilde{A}_{2}(x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq|x|^{-1}}|\widehat{s}_{k}|\leq\frac{\rho}{L^{3}}\sum_{\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq|x|^{-1}}\frac{1}{k^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\rho}{|x|}

and

|x^{2}B(x)|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}\big\|\delta_{j}^{2}\big((\chi-\chi_{|x|^{-1}})\widehat{s}\big)\big\|_{1}\leq\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{|x|^{-1}\leq|k|\leq 2}\frac{\rho}{|k|^{4}}\lesssim\rho|x|\,.

As for
C(x)
, we can apply again (
A.12
), which holds true for all
|x|\geq 2\mathfrak{a}
.
This shows (
A.13
) and concludes the proof of the first bound in (
2.23
), which, in turn, immediately implies the first two bounds in (
2.24
).

Next, we prove that, for
r>\mathfrak{a}
,

\displaystyle\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))|\nabla s(x)|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\left\{r^{-4}\rho^{\frac{1}{2}},r^{-1}\rho^{2}\right\}\,.

(A.14)

To show (
A.14
), first in the case
r\leq\rho^{-1/2}
, we define the coefficients
\widehat{s}_{r}(k)=\widehat{s}(k)\chi_{r^{-1}}(k)
(recall that
\chi_{r^{-1}}(k)=\chi(kr)
and
\chi\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, with
\chi(x)=1
for
|x|<2
,
\chi(x)=0
, for
|x|>4
). We denote by
s_{r}
the periodic function, with coefficients
\widehat{s}_{r}(k)
and we decompose

\begin{split}\int(1&-\chi_{r}(x))|\nabla s(x)|^{2}dx\\
\lesssim\;&\int|\nabla s_{r}(x)|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\int x^{2}|\nabla(s-s_{r})(x)|^{2}dx\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}k^{2}|\widehat{s}_{r}(k)|^{2}+\frac{1}{L^{3}r^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}\big|\delta_{j}\big(k\widehat{s}(k)(1-\chi_{r^{-1}}(k))\big)\big|^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{L^{3}}\sum_{|k|\leq 2r^{-1}}k^{2}|\widehat{s}(k)|^{2}\\
&+\frac{1}{L^{3}r^{2}}\Big[\sum_{j=1}^{3}\sum_{|k|\geq r^{-1}}k^{2}|(\delta_{j}\widehat{s})_{k}|^{2}+\sum_{|k|\geq r^{-1}}|\widehat{s}_{k}|^{2}+r^{2}\sum_{r^{-1}\leq|k|\leq 5r^{-1}}k^{2}|\widehat{s}_{k}|^{2}\Big]\,.\end{split}

(A.15)

With the bounds (
A.8
) and recalling that
r\leq\rho^{-1/2}
, we obtain

\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))|\nabla s(x)|^{2}dx\lesssim\rho^{2}/r\,.

If
r\geq\rho^{-1/2}
, we proceed similarly, but we estimate
1-\chi_{r}(x)\lesssim|x|^{6}/r^{6}
(rather than
1-\chi_{r}(x)\lesssim x^{2}/r^{2}
, as we did in (
A.15
)); this leads to

\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))|\nabla s(x)|^{2}dx\lesssim\rho^{1/2}/r^{4}

as claimed. In particular, choosing
r=\ell_{0}
, (
A.14
) implies the third bound in (
2.24
).

Analogously to (
A.14
), we also find the useful bounds

\displaystyle\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))\left|\Delta s(x)\right|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{-3}\rho^{2},

(A.16)

\displaystyle\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))\left|\nabla\Delta s(x)\right|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{-5}\rho^{2},

(A.17)

for all
r>\mathfrak{a}
. To show (
A.16
), we let
\widehat{A}_{r}(k):=\big(k^{2}\widehat{s}(k)+\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)/2\big)\chi_{r^{-1}}(k)
and we compute (recalling that
V_{\text{eff}}(x)=0
for
|x|>\mathfrak{a}
)

\displaystyle\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))

\displaystyle\left|\Delta s(x)\right|^{2}dx

\displaystyle=\int(1-\chi_{r}(x))\big|\Delta s(x)+\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}V_{\mathrm{eff}}(x)\big|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\widehat{A}_{r}\|_{2}^{2}+r^{-4}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\big\|\delta_{j}^{2}\big(|k|^{2}\widehat{s}(k)+\frac{\rho_{0}}{2}\widehat{V}_{\mathrm{eff}}(k)-\widehat{A}_{r}(k)\big)\big\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim r^{-3}\rho^{2}\,.

As for (
A.17
), we proceed similarly, using
1-\chi_{r}(x)\leq x^{6}/r^{6}
to bound the contribution from high momenta
|k|\gtrsim r^{-1}
.

Let us now consider (
2.25
). First of all, we notice that, from (
A.2
)-(
A.4
),

\|s\|_{2}^{2}=L^{-3}\sum_{k\in\Lambda^{*}}|\widehat{s}(k)|^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2}.

With the definition (
2.19
) of
\tilde{\sigma}
, the fact that
1-C\mathfrak{a}/\ell\leq f_{\ell}(x)\leq 1
on the support of
\tilde{\sigma}
and the bound

\|\omega_{\ell}\|^{2}_{2}\lesssim\int_{|x|\leq C\ell}|x|^{-2}dx\lesssim\ell

we obtain

\|\tilde{\sigma}\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{2}\|\omega_{\ell}\|_{2}^{2}+\|s\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2}

which shows the first bound in (
2.25
), for
\zeta=\tilde{\sigma}
. To prove it for
\zeta=\sigma
, it is convenient to first show (
2.29
). To this end, we compute

\displaystyle\int_{|x|\leq\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}}|\tilde{\sigma}(x)|dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho^{-\frac{3}{4}}\|\tilde{\sigma}\|_{2}\lesssim 1

(A.18)

and, by (
A.10
),

\displaystyle\int_{|x|\geq\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}}|\tilde{\sigma}(x)|dx=\int_{\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}<|x|\leq 2\ell_{0}}|\tilde{\sigma}(x)|dx\lesssim\rho^{1/4}\int_{\rho^{-\frac{1}{2}}<|x|\leq 2\ell_{0}}|x|^{-5/2}dx\lesssim\big(\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\ell_{0}\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

This establishes (
2.29
), from which

\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\|\tilde{\sigma}\|_{2}^{2}+\ell_{0}^{-3}\|\tilde{\sigma}\|_{1}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2}

(A.19)

proving (
2.25
), for
\zeta=\sigma
. Since, in Fourier space,
1\leq\widehat{\gamma}_{k}=\cosh(\widehat{\eta}_{k})\leq 1+|\sinh(\widehat{\eta}_{k})|=1+|\widehat{\sigma}_{k}|
, (
A.19
) also implies
\|\zeta\|_{2}^{2}\lesssim\rho^{3/2}
for
\zeta=\{\gamma-\mathbbm{1},\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1},\gamma^{-1}*\sigma\}
.

We proceed similarly to show the first bound in (
2.26
). Again, we start with
\zeta=\tilde{\sigma}
. In this case, the first estimate in (
2.26
) follows from (
A.14
), together with
\|\nabla f_{\ell}\|_{2}=\|\nabla\omega_{\ell}\|_{2}\lesssim 1
and with the first two bounds in (
2.24
) (to handle terms involving the derivative of the cutoffs).
With (
2.29
), we conclude that the first bound in (
2.26
) also holds for
\zeta=\sigma
and therefore, since
0\leq\widehat{\gamma}_{k}-1\leq|\widehat{\sigma}_{k}|
, also for
\zeta=\{\gamma-\mathbbm{1},\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1},\gamma^{-1}*\sigma\}
.

The second bound in (
2.25
) follows from (
2.27
), taking
m=0
. Also the third bound in (
2.25
) follows from (
2.27
), since

\displaystyle\int|\zeta(x)|dx

\displaystyle=\int_{|x|\leq\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\ell_{0}^{2}}|\zeta(x)|dx+\int_{|x|>\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\ell_{0}^{2}}|\zeta(x)|dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\rho^{\frac{3}{4}}\ell_{0}^{3}\|\zeta\|_{2}+\int_{|x|>\rho^{\frac{1}{2}}\ell_{0}^{2}}\frac{\rho}{\ell}\Big(\frac{\rho^{\frac{1}{4}}\ell_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{|x|}\Big)^{m}dx\lesssim\rho^{\frac{3}{2}}\ell_{0}^{3}

where we have used the
L^{2}
-estimate in (
2.25
) and we have chosen
m
sufficiently large.

Let us now show (
2.27
). First, we claim that

\left\|\delta^{m}_{j}\widehat{\sigma}\right\|_{1}\lesssim\frac{\rho}{\ell}\ell_{0}^{m}

(A.20)

for
j=1,2,3
and
m\in\mathbb{N}
. To show (
A.20
), we first observe, using (
2.29
) and simple scaling, that

\left\|\delta^{m}_{j}\widehat{\sigma}\right\|_{1}\lesssim\big\|\delta^{m}_{j}\widehat{\tilde{\sigma}}\big\|_{1}+\ell_{0}^{m}\rho^{3/2}.

To estimate
\|\delta^{m}_{j}\widehat{\tilde{\sigma}}\|_{1}
, we use Cauchy-Schwarz with the bound
\|(\ell|k|+\ell^{2}k^{2})^{-1}\|_{2}\lesssim\ell^{-3/2}
and then we switch to position space. Since
\widetilde{\sigma}(x)
is supported on
|x|\leq 2\ell_{0}
, we find

\big\|\delta^{m}_{j}\widehat{\tilde{\sigma}}\big\|_{1}\lesssim\ell^{-\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{\alpha=1}^{2}\ell^{\alpha}\big\||k|^{\alpha}\delta^{m}_{j}\widehat{\tilde{\sigma}}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\ell_{0}^{m}\sum_{\alpha=1}^{2}\ell^{-\frac{3}{2}+\alpha}\big\|\nabla^{\alpha}{\tilde{\sigma}}\big\|_{2}+\ell_{0}^{m-1}\ell^{-1/2}\|\tilde{\sigma}\|_{2}\,.

(A.21)

From the definition (
2.19
) of
\tilde{\sigma}
, we have

\begin{split}|\nabla^{\alpha}\tilde{\sigma}(x)|\lesssim\;&\chi_{\ell_{0}}(2x)(1-\chi_{\ell}(2x))\Big(|\nabla^{\alpha}s(x)|+\rho|\nabla^{\alpha}\omega_{\ell}(x)|\Big)\\
&+\bigg|\nabla^{\alpha}\frac{\chi_{\ell_{0}}(2x)(1-\chi_{\ell}(2x))}{f_{\ell}(x)}\bigg|\big(\rho|\omega_{\ell}(x)|+|s(x)|\big)\,.\end{split}

(A.22)

We can bound the norm of the contributions in the first line observing that, for
\alpha=1,2
,

\big\|(1-\chi_{\ell}(2\cdot))\nabla^{\alpha}\omega_{\ell}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\ell^{1/2-\alpha}

and, by (
A.14
) and (
A.16
),

\begin{split}\big\|\chi_{\ell_{0}}(2\cdot)(1-\chi_{\ell}(2\cdot))\nabla^{\alpha}s\big\|^{2}_{2}\lesssim\;&\ell^{1-2\alpha}\rho^{2}.\end{split}

(A.23)

As for the last term in (
A.22
), we notice that

\bigg|\nabla^{\alpha}\frac{\chi_{\ell_{0}}(2x)(1-\chi_{\ell}(2x))}{f_{\ell}(x)}\bigg|\lesssim\ell_{0}^{-\alpha}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{0}/2\leq|x|\leq\ell_{0}}+\ell^{-\alpha}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell/2\leq|x|\leq 2\ell}\,.

(A.24)

On the one hand, we have, for
\alpha=1,2
,

\ell_{0}^{-\alpha}\big\|\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{0}/2\leq|\cdot|\leq\ell_{0}}\big(\rho|\omega_{\ell}|+|s|\big)\big\|_{2}\lesssim\rho^{5/4}\,.

On the other hand, applying Hölder’s and Sobolev’s inequalities as well as (
A.23
) and the first bound in (
2.25
), we find

\begin{split}\ell^{-\alpha}\big\|\mathbbm{1}_{\ell/2\leq|\cdot|\leq 2\ell}&\big(\rho|\omega_{\ell}|+|s|\big)\big\|_{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\ell^{1/2-\alpha}\rho+\ell^{-\alpha}\big\|\mathbbm{1}_{\ell/2\leq|\cdot|\leq 2\ell}\big\|_{3}\big\|\mathbbm{1}_{\ell/2\leq|\cdot|\leq 2\ell}\,s\big\|_{6}\\
\lesssim\;&\ell^{1/2-\alpha}\rho+\ell^{-\alpha+1}\bigg(\int_{\ell/2\leq|x|\leq 2\ell}\big(|\nabla s(x)|^{2}+\ell^{-2}|s(x)|^{2}\big)dx\bigg)^{1/2}\lesssim\ell^{1/2-\alpha}\rho\,.\end{split}

Thus, we obtain

\big\|\nabla^{\alpha}\tilde{\sigma}\big\|_{2}\lesssim\ell^{1/2-\alpha}\rho\,.

Inserting in (
A.21
) and using the
L^{2}
-bound in (
2.25
), we find

\|\delta_{j}^{m}\widehat{\tilde{\sigma}}\|_{1}\lesssim\frac{\rho}{\ell}\ell_{0}^{m}

(A.25)

and thus (
A.20
), for
j=1,2,3
and all
m\in\mathbb{N}
.

In particular, (
A.25
) and (
A.20
) imply that
\|\widehat{\zeta}\|_{1}\lesssim\rho/\ell
, for every
\zeta\in\{\tilde{\sigma},\sigma,\gamma-\mathbbm{1},\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1},\gamma^{-1}*\sigma\}
, since
1\leq\widehat{\gamma}_{k}\leq 1+|\widehat{\sigma}_{k}|
, for all
k\in\Lambda^{*}
. Hence

|\zeta(x)|\lesssim\big\|\widehat{\zeta}\big\|_{1}\lesssim\ell^{-1}\rho

which shows the validity of (
2.27
), for
m=0
. To verify (
2.27
) in the case
m>0
, we introduce the functions
h_{1}(t):=\sqrt{1+t^{2}}-1,h_{2}(t)=1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+t^{2}}}
and
h_{3}(t):=t/(1+t^{2})^{1/2}
so that
\widehat{\gamma}_{k}-1=h_{1}(\widehat{\sigma}_{k})
,
1-1/\widehat{\gamma}_{k}=h_{2}(\widehat{\sigma}_{k})
and
\widehat{\gamma}^{-1}_{k}\widehat{\sigma}_{k}=h_{3}(\widehat{\sigma}_{k})
. Since
h_{1},h_{2},h_{3}
are smooth, with bounded derivatives, we conclude, by the chain rule, that

\big|(\delta_{j}^{m}h_{u}(\widehat{\sigma}))_{k}\big|\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta_{1},\dots,\beta_{i}\geq 1:\\
\beta_{1}+\dots+\beta_{i}=m\end{subarray}}\prod_{r=1}^{i}|(\delta_{j}^{\beta_{r}}\widehat{\sigma})_{k}|

for
u=1,2,3
. Therefore, we obtain

\|\delta_{j}^{m}\widehat{\zeta}\|_{1}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\beta_{1},\dots,\beta_{i}\geq 1:\\
\beta_{1}+\dots+\beta_{i}=m\end{subarray}}\|\delta^{\beta_{1}}\widehat{\sigma}\|_{1}\prod_{r=2}^{i}\|\delta_{j}^{\beta_{r}}\widehat{\sigma}\|_{\infty}

for
\zeta\in\{\tilde{\sigma},\sigma,\gamma-\mathbbm{1},\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1},\gamma^{-1}*\sigma\}
.
With (
2.29
), we have

\|\delta_{j}^{n}\widehat{\sigma}\|_{\infty}\lesssim\||x_{j}|^{n}\sigma\|_{1}\lesssim\ell_{0}^{n}\|\sigma\|_{1}\lesssim\ell_{0}^{n}(\rho^{1/2}\ell_{0})^{1/2}\,.

Together with (
A.20
), we conclude that

\|\delta_{j}^{m}\widehat{\zeta}\|_{1}\leq\frac{\rho}{\ell}(\rho^{1/2}\ell^{3}_{0})^{m/2}\,.

From (
A.1
), this leads to

|x_{j}|^{m}|\zeta(x)|\lesssim\frac{\rho}{\ell}(\rho^{1/4}\ell^{3/2}_{0})^{m}

for
j=1,2,3
and
m\in\mathbb{N}
, which implies (
2.27
).

The proof of the second bound in (
2.26
) is obtained replicating the proof of (
2.27
) for
m=0
, starting with the analogous of (
A.20
), with
k\widehat{\sigma}(k)
replacing
\widehat{\sigma}(k)
and using in addition (
A.17
).

As for (
2.28
), it follows easily from the first two bounds in (
2.24
), recalling the definitions (
2.19
), (
2.20
) of
\tilde{\sigma},\sigma
(and the bound (
2.29
)).

Next, let us show the second bound in (
2.23
), establishing the decay of
|\nabla s(x)|
.
To this end, we introduce

D(x)=-\frac{\rho_{0}}{8\pi}\int\frac{\chi_{\mu}(x-y)}{|x-y|}V_{\text{eff}}(y)dy=\frac{\rho_{0}}{4\pi\mathfrak{a}}\int_{\partial B_{\mathfrak{a}}(0)}\frac{\chi_{\mu}(x-y)}{|x-y|}dy

(A.26)

for a fixed
\mu>2\mathfrak{a}
. We also define
E(x)=s(x)-D(x)
for all
x\in\Lambda
.
In view of (
A.6
) and keeping in mind that

\frac{4\pi}{k^{2}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dx}{|x|}\,e^{ik\cdot x}

(A.27)

we expect
D
to be a good approximation of
s
, at high momenta (and therefore
\widehat{E}_{k}
to exhibit fast decay in
k
). With

\widehat{D}_{k}=-\frac{\rho_{0}}{8\pi}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)\int dx\,\frac{\chi_{\mu}(x)}{|x|}e^{-ik\cdot x}

we can easily bound

|(\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\widehat{D})_{k}|\lesssim\rho|k|^{-\alpha}

(A.28)

for
j=1,2,3
and
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}
. At high momenta
|k|\geq 1
, we can use (
A.27
) to write

\widehat{D}_{k}=-\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}{2k^{2}}+\frac{\rho_{0}}{8\pi}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dx\frac{(1-\chi_{\mu}(x))}{|x|}e^{-ik\cdot x}

(A.29)

and derive the improved estimate

\Big|\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\big[\widehat{D}_{k}+\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}{2k^{2}}\big]\Big|\lesssim\rho|k|^{-m}

(A.30)

for
j=1,2,3
and any
m\in\mathbb{N}
(in the integral on the r.h.s. of (
A.29
), we can use the smoothness of
(1-\chi_{\mu}(x)/|x|
to integrate by parts and obtain arbitrarily power-law decay in
k
). Combining (
A.8
) with (
A.28
), we find

|(\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\widehat{E})_{k}|\leq|(\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\widehat{s})_{k}|+|(\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\widehat{D})_{k}|\lesssim\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\rho^{1/4}|k|^{-\alpha-1/2}&\text{if $|k|\leq\rho^{1/2}$}\\
\rho|k|^{-\alpha-2}&\text{if $\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq 1$}\,.\end{array}\right.

Combining instead (
A.9
) with (
A.30
), we obtain

|(\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\widehat{E})_{k}|\leq\Big|\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\big[\widehat{s}_{k}+\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}{2k^{2}}\big]\Big|+\Big|\delta_{j}^{\alpha}\big[\widehat{D}_{k}+\frac{\rho_{0}\widehat{V}_{\text{eff}}(k)}{2k^{2}}\big]\Big|\lesssim\frac{\rho^{2}}{|k|^{6}}+\frac{\rho}{|k|^{m}}

for
|k|\geq 1
and for any
m\in\mathbb{N}
. Hence, we conclude that

\begin{split}\Big|x^{2}\nabla E(x)|&\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\delta_{j}^{2}k\widehat{E}_{k}\|_{1}\\
&\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|k\delta_{j}^{2}\widehat{E}_{k}\|_{1}+\|\delta_{j}\widehat{E}\|_{1}\\
&\lesssim\frac{1}{L^{3}}\Big[\sum_{|k|\leq\rho^{1/2}}\frac{\rho^{1/4}}{|k|^{3/2}}+\sum_{\rho^{1/2}\leq|k|\leq 1}\frac{\rho}{|k|^{3}}+\sum_{|k|\geq 1}\frac{\rho}{|k|^{5}}\Big]\lesssim\rho|\log\rho|\,.\end{split}

On the other hand, for
|x|>2\mathfrak{a}
, (
A.26
) implies, by explicit computation, that
|\nabla D(x)|\lesssim\rho|x|^{-2}
. This completes the proof of the second bound in (
2.23
).

Recalling the definitions (
2.19
), (
2.20
) of
\tilde{\sigma},\sigma
and the bounds (
A.13
), (
A.24
), we also obtain (
2.30
). In turn, this implies (
2.31
). As for (
2.32
), it follows from

\|\nabla(\gamma^{-1}*\sigma)\|_{p}=\|\gamma^{-1}*\nabla\sigma\|_{p}\leq\|(\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1})*\nabla\sigma\|_{p}+\|\nabla\sigma\|_{p}\leq\|\nabla\sigma\|_{p}\big(1+\|\mathbbm{1}-\gamma^{-1}\|_{1}\big)

applying (
2.31
) and the
L^{1}
-bound in (
2.25
).
∎
